## ⭐⭐ SN4-RTX — TOTAL RUNTIME REDESIGN IN OPTIMIZED x86 ASM (Lon PIVOT directive, s162 2026-07-25)
**RULING OF RECORD (promotes the s161 caveat to head rung):** the C runtime is a TRANSITIONAL ARTIFACT. The target SNOBOL4 runtime is hand-written, register-aware x86-64 assembly. We use REGISTERS freely; System V conventions bind ONLY at (a) libc call boundaries and (b) the m3 driver→blob entry edge. References: **SPITBOL x64 asm runtime** (`/home/claude/x64` — Minimal registers pinned to hard regs: IA=r12 W0=rax WA=rcx WB=rbx WC=rdx XL=rsi XR=rdi CP=r13 RA=xmm12; C-boundary = save-all-to-global-block/call/restore, `int.asm`; the runtime itself = `bootstrap/sbl.asm`, 19,064 lines) · **CSNOBOL4** C runtime (semantics reference; `git clone https://github.com/snobol4ever/csnobol4`, build via `scripts/build_csnobol4_oracle.sh`) · **SIL** macro source (algorithm reference — locate/fetch at need).
**RTX-0a MEASURED SURFACE (s162, static call sites in `claws5.s` — matches the s161 dynamic anatomy):** `str_concat_d`×38 · `rt_subscript_var`×33 (TABLE) · `rt_deref`×24 · `rt_call_arr`×22 + `rt_proc_call_epilogue_`×14 + `rt_proc_open_fn`×8 (the NRETURN/call path) · `rt_num_arith`×8 · `rt_binop_overload`×8 · `rt_defer_step`/`rt_dcap_step`×6 each · `rt_coerce_num2_d`×6.   **⭐ TWO IEEE TRAPS FOUND AND HANDLED IN `rt_is_truthy` — the reason a bit-test would have been WRONG: `-0.0` is bitwise NONZERO but `-0.0 != 0.0` is FALSE (so falsy), and `NaN != 0.0` is TRUE (so truthy). Implemented as `ucomisd` + `setne` OR `setp`. Both are in the unit battery.**

  **⭐⭐ FALSIFICATION GATE (the proof that matters, and the pattern EVERY later rung must repeat): a passing differential test CANNOT distinguish "asm is correct" from "gate is stuck OFF and C did the work." ## ⛔ FACT RULE — `.ref` FILES ARE THE **TESTING** ORACLE; THE BIG `.dat`/`.json` ARE THE **BENCHMARK** INPUT. THEY ARE TWO DIFFERENT THINGS (Lon, 2026-07-25 s161)
**The `.ref` files ARE CORRECT. Do not "regenerate" them against the large corpus inputs.** Each demo family has TWO distinct uses and they must never be conflated:
- **TESTING** — a small, hand-sized input whose expected output is the committed `.ref`. This is the correctness gate: byte-identity of stdout. It is small BY DESIGN so it is fast, reviewable, and CI-safe.
- **BENCHMARKING** — the large committed inputs (`CLAWS5inTASA.dat` 67 KB, `VBGinTASA.dat` 100 KB, `citm_catalog.json` 1.73 MB, `twitter.json` 632 KB, `calculator.input` 2,208 exprs). These exist to produce TIMING, and they have NO `.ref`.
⚠ THE TRAP THAT CAUGHT s161: running a work-horse against the BENCHMARK input and diffing against the TESTING `.ref` produces a huge bogus diff (claws5 95-line ref vs 5,617-line benchmark output; treebank 24 vs 8,727) whose first ~94 lines MATCH — which looks exactly like a "stale/truncated ref" and invites a destructive regeneration. It is neither stale nor truncated: it is the answer to a DIFFERENT QUESTION. **Before concluding a `.ref` is wrong, check which input it belongs to.** Correctness for the benchmark inputs is established engine-to-engine (sbl vs m3 vs m4 byte-identity), not against a `.ref`.

## s161 — TIME() INSTRUMENTATION, FENCE PARITY, json.sno, AND THE CLAWS5 ANATOMY
**⭐ `json.sno` WRITTEN — the missing 6th work-horse.** RFC-8259 de-serializer into native structures: object→TABLE (in a `jobj` DATA wrapper that also keeps an insertion-ordered key ARRAY so output is deterministic), array→ARRAY(1..n), number→INTEGER or REAL via CONVERT, string→STRING with `\uXXXX`→UTF-8. Validated against an independent parser: **all 14 datatype counts exact on both twitter.json and citm_catalog.json**. Shift/reduce value stack (frame records stack base; close-brace reduces) so cost is O(nodes) with no per-frame allocation. **ALL deferred actions are the DIRECT `epsilon . *f()` form — none built through EVAL**, deliberately, so json.sno is not a sixth victim of the s160b EVAL/deferred slot collision.
**⭐ FOCUS GROUP IS 16: 6 work-horse + 5 match + 5 match-fence** (treebank has two work-horses, list and array, but only one match/match-fence pair). Note `json-match.sno` and `json-match-fence.sno` are **byte-identical files** — json's grammar already carries FENCE1 in `ws`/`jnumber`.
**⭐ TIME() BRACKETS ON ALL 6 WORK-HORSES, WRITTEN TO `TERMINAL` (= stderr on BOTH engines, verified).** stdout therefore stays byte-comparable against `.ref`; the non-deterministic timing can never pollute a testing oracle. Single-match programs bracket the one match; loop programs accumulate match-only time excluding INPUT/OUTPUT. `TIME()` = ms of compute since program start, Unix excludes I/O wait (manual p.229).
**⭐ FENCE PARITY LANDED — each work-horse now mirrors ITS OWN counterpart, which is not uniform:** claws5 uses the **bare FENCE (FENCE0)** at ARBNO-iteration head, because that is what `claws5-match-fence.sno` uses — NOT FENCE1. treebank-list/array take FENCE1 on `group` and on the `*group|word` alternation. calculator-1 (right recursion) fences every level. **calculator-2 fences ONLY atom/factor — its ARBNO operator loops are FENCE-HOSTILE (s148); T/X left unfenced deliberately.**
**⭐ CALCULATORS RESTRUCTURED TO ONE BIG MATCH + THE MISSING FENCE0 TEST (Lon directive).** Both now slurp every expression into one multi-line string and match once: `eol = CHAR(10) FENCE` / `C = POS(0) ARBNO(X eol (epsilon . *EMIT())) RPOS(0)`. FENCE0 sits at the newline so each completed line commits and can never be backtracked into (manual p.125: FENCE matches null forward, FAILS the match on backup, anchors when first). New `EMIT()` prints the line value and resets the operand stack. **Both calculators remain BYTE-IDENTICAL to their `.ref`.** This is the FENCE0 coverage the corpus was missing.
**⭐ TWO SILENT BREAKAGES FIXED.** (a) **claws5 never matched at all**: `&TRIM` unset ⇒ defaults to 1 ⇒ strips each line's trailing space ⇒ `src = src line` merged the last token of a line into the first of the next. Fixed `&TRIM = 0` + nl-joined slurp + `SPAN(' ' CHAR(10))` separator, matching its `-match` sibling. (b) **BOTH treebanks failed to COMPILE under sbl** — five ERROR 217 duplicate-label + SEGV, because `init_list`/`Init_list` (and 4 more pairs) collide under default case-folding. Fixed with a leading `-CASE 0` control line; verified BOTH engines accept it and honour case-sensitivity (manual p.172). SCRIP is always case-sensitive so it is a no-op there.
**⭐ SLURP IS O(n²) IF WRITTEN NAIVELY — 3-LEVEL ACCUMULATOR IS MANDATORY FOR THE BENCHMARK INPUTS.** `src = src line` on 1.7 MB costs **11,873 ms**; an 8 KB→256 KB→full three-level accumulator costs **145 ms (82×)**. Adopted in json.sno + claws5 + both calculators.
**⭐ NEW SCRIP DEFECT (isolated, 8-line repro): ASSOCIATED INPUT NEVER SIGNALS EOF.** `INPUT(.RAW, 9, '[-f0 -r1000000]')` read in a loop fails correctly at EOF under sbl; under scrip the associated variable NEVER fails ⇒ infinite loop. Plain `INPUT` is correct on both. Repro `/tmp/p2.sno` shape. **Consequence beyond the hang: a SINGLE raw read silently TRUNCATES at the -r length — `json-match.sno` reads only 1,000,000 of citm_catalog.json's 1,727,204 bytes and reports "Pattern match failed" as if the JSON were malformed.** Owed: fix the EOF signal, then json-match/-match-fence can go back to the fast 2-read path.
**⭐⭐ CLAWS5 ANATOMY — THE GAP IS A BROAD RUNTIME CONSTANT, *NOT* THE PATTERN ENGINE AND *NOT* THE ARBNO/DEFER CEREMONY.** Three-way ablation at 133 KB (same program, semantic actions removed layer by layer; RT_OPT=**-O0**):
| layer | sbl | m3 | ratio on that layer |
|---|---|---|---|
| A match only (no calls) | 1 ms | 5 ms | 5.0× |
| B + deferred `epsilon . *f()` calls, no table work | 7 ms | 30 ms | **4.2×** |
| C + full nested TABLE work (`add_tok` ≈ 11 subscripts/token) | 15 ms | 71 ms | **5.1×** |
⇒ **SCRIP is ~4–5× slower UNIFORMLY across all three layers — there is no single elephant for claws5.** Semantic actions are 93% of total time on BOTH engines (14/15 sbl, 66/71 m3), i.e. the two engines spend the SAME PROPORTION on work vs matching; the pattern engine is not the problem here. This DISTINGUISHES claws5 from treebank/calc-2, whose heatmaps DO concentrate in PAT$_α ceremony (s148). **The claws5 lever is the RUNTIME — the NRETURN function-call path and TABLE subscript/hash — not the emitter.** ⚠ **CAVEAT UPDATED (Lon directive, 2026-07-25 s161): DO NOT pursue `-O2` C runtime to close this gap. The C runtime is a TRANSITIONAL ARTIFACT — the target runtime is x86 ASSEMBLY, register-aware, NOT C-optimized.** The 4–5× uniform gap for claws5's TABLE/NRETURN layers is a consequence of C code that will be REPLACED by hand-written x86 runtime (same architectural move that already makes the match layer competitive). **The right question is NOT "-O2 C?" — it is "implement the NRETURN call path and TABLE subscript in the x86 asm runtime."** Lon directive: do not re-open the -O2 question on claws5's behalf in any session. The O2-DIRECTED-ONLY rule (s126) stays in force for benchmarking; the gap-closure path is x86 asm runtime.
**BOARD (match-only ms, RT_OPT=-O0, benchmark inputs, sbl vs m3, all five sbl/m3 BYTE-IDENTICAL):** claws5 7/35 · treebank-list 21/168 · **treebank-array 736/229 = 3.2× FASTER (already past target)** · calculator-1 16/55 · calculator-2 18/59 · json 70/381. treebank-array's win is partly SPITBOL doing badly (736 ms vs list's 21 ms on the same input — that variant allocates a TABLE per stack frame).
**NOT COMMITTED — no push attempted this session.**
 — **`src/` CHANGED (lower only). ⚠ `.s` REGEN IS OWED (RULES step 4 FIRES — codegen changed).**
**⭐ LON DIRECTIVE EXECUTED: `sno_cconst_lookup` IS ERADICATED.** *"Not a valid optimization. We will only fold the actual literal constants, `CHAR(literal)`, and `&LCASE`, and `&UCASE`. Do not go look up other code variables and assume they are constant."* `sno_cset_fold` now admits EXACTLY: `TT_QLIT` · `TT_KEYWORD` ∈ {`&LCASE`,`&UCASE`} ONLY · `CHAR(n)` with n an integer LITERAL 1..255 · `TT_SEQ`/`TT_CAT` recursion. **The `TT_VAR` arm is GONE**, and with it the whole dead `sno_cconst_*` subsystem (write/scan_writes/scan_indirect_target/note_define_names/build_table/lookup + 3 call sites + the table). `lower_snobol4.c` **2,440 → 2,387 lines**; `grep -c 'sno_cconst\|SNO_CCONST'` = **0**. (`SNO_CCONST_MAX` had been doubling as the freeze-write bound — that got its own `SNO_FZW_MAX` instead of a dangling misnomer.)

Blast radius measured BEFORE cutting: one corpus file (`csnobol4-suite/digits.sno`) mentions any of them, as `OUTPUT = &DIGITS` — **not a cset arg**, so the fold never sees it.
**⭐ WHY s160a's CORPUS REWRITE WAS A STRICT PREREQUISITE — MEASURED BOTH WAYS.** REWRITTEN demos under the NEW fold are **byte-identical** to before (claws5 149,023 · treebank-list 217,474 · calc-1 149,714 · calc-2 150,015) ⇒ the rewrite fully substituted for the unsound arm. The PRE-REWRITE ORIGINALS under the new fold **GROW**: claws5 **+1,434 B** · calc-1 **+1,093 B** · calc-2 **+1,093 B** (they *were* leaning on the unsound lookup) · treebank-list **+0** (capture-assigned `nl` never folded anyway — the s160a mechanism). ⇒ literalize the corpus FIRST, then remove the arm at zero codegen cost.

Beauty 39/3/3, the 3 are `trace --run` and unrelated (`sno_cset_fold` is file-`static`, **zero** refs outside `lower_snobol4.c`, cannot reach snocone). RT_OPT=-O0.
**⭐⭐ TREEBANK m4 ROOT-CAUSED — 15-LINE REPRO, NO CORPUS NEEDED.** m4 emits 17 B (`('NNP', ('NNP'))`) vs m3's correct 281,844; at 400 B input, m3 1,086 B vs m4 15 B — **the bank root is a leaf tag, not `BANK`** ⇒ frames never pop. EXCLUDED BY MEASUREMENT (do not re-walk): **slurp + `spat` match loops are BIT-IDENTICAL m3/m4** (probe: `SIZE(src)=3001`, matches 1–4 identical item sizes 367/450/400/378) · **`nl` is correct in m4** (`SIZE(nl)=1` in sbl/m3/m4) · not the cset fold. **THE DEFECT: in mode 4, a DIRECT-built `epsilon . *f()` deferred-side-effect pattern constructed AFTER an `EVAL()`-built one BINDS TO THE EVAL-BUILT PATTERN'S DEFERRED EXPRESSION.** Repro prints `SIDE-EFFECT HELLO` where sbl and m3 both print `DIRECT-EFFECT`. **TWO CONTROLS PIN IT: (a) two DIRECT forms, no EVAL ⇒ m4 CORRECT; (b) reverse order, DIRECT built first then EVAL ⇒ m4 CORRECT.** ⇒ ORDER-DEPENDENT and EVAL-TRIGGERED — the run-time-compile path appears to latch a deferred/capture binding that the next compile-time-built deferred target reuses instead of allocating its own. **MAPS 1:1 ONTO TREEBANK:** `Init_list`/`Push_list`/`Push_item`/`Pop_final` are EVAL-built (l.57/64/71/85) and **`Pop_list` is the DIRECT form built LAST (l.78)** ⇒ `Pop_list()` fires the wrong callback ⇒ frames never pop ⇒ single-node bank. `treebank-list` is the same collision with a different residue (`''`). ## s159 CURSOR (superseded by s160 above) — **NO `src/` CHANGE; MEASUREMENT + HARNESS ONLY. No `.s` regen owed (RULES step 4 does not fire).**
**⛔⛔ TWO LON RULINGS THIS SESSION — BOTH REVERSE EARLIER PLANNING, READ FIRST:**
**(1) THE VAR CSET FOLD IS DANGEROUS — DO NOT RELY ON IT.** `sno_cset_fold` resolves a bare variable (`SPAN(DIGITS)`) via `sno_cconst_lookup`, which admits on `total==1 && clean==1` (assigned exactly once, cleanly). That analysis is NOT sound in SNOBOL4: indirect assignment (`$('DIG' 'ITS')`), immediate assignment in a pattern, later fragments, and `CODE()` run-time compilation can all write the name after the fold decided it was constant. Lon s159: "that optimization is dangerous — would not be prudent." USE `&LCASE`/`&UCASE` — PROTECTED keywords the program cannot assign, so folding them is sound. ⚠ `&DIGITS` is NOT a standard SPITBOL keyword (SCRIP's fold table has a `digits` entry anyway) — keep digits as the literal `'0123456789'` or the rewrite compiles under SCRIP and dies under sbl.
**(2) FENCE IS NOT AN OPTIMIZATION AGAINST SPITBOL — HEAT-0 "AUTOMATIC FENCE ADMISSION" IS WITHDRAWN.** FENCE PRUNES THE SEARCH SPACE: the fenced program is a DIFFERENT program, and the plain program's semantics REQUIRE the backtracking FENCE removes, so no compiler may insert it. The plain-vs-fence pair is a **DIAGNOSTIC ONLY** — it localizes SCRIP's loss to BACKTRACKING. The work is MAKING BACKTRACKING CHEAP, never removing it. s157's "HEAT-0 gets treebank to 0.76" is VOID.
**⭐ CSET FOLD ALREADY FIRES — PROVEN, NOT INFERRED.** Rewrote csets to literals and diffed `--compile` output: claws5 (`SPAN(DIGITS)`/`ANY(UCASE)`/`SPAN(DIGITS UCASE)`, 3 subs) ⇒ **byte-identical, 149,023 B**; calculator-2 (`ANY(&LCASE)`/`ANY(LCASE)`/`SPAN(DIGITS)`, 3 subs) ⇒ **byte-identical**. `sno_cset_fold` (lower_snobol4.c:890) covers QLIT / KEYWORD / `CHAR(n)`-int / VAR-via-cconst / `TT_CAT` recursion, gated at :964 on ANY|NOTANY|SPAN|BREAK|BREAKX. ⇒ A literal-concatenation SOURCE REWRITE BUYS ZERO SPEED. Its only value is INSURANCE against a future edit breaking the fold — and per ruling (1) the keyword form is the sound way to get it.

## ⭐⭐ DEFER-BIND LADDER (mint s149, Lon eureka + Opus code verification; supersedes the bare "ARBNO/DEFER ceremony" head from s148)

**THE EUREKA (Lon, s149):** bind every defer-of-VAR site directly to the variable's CURRENT value and FIX UP all sites on write. 99.999% of programs use DEFER purely as forward reference to an init-once pattern variable (VERIFIED for the 5 demos: every deferred name — calc F/T/X, treebank group, json jelement/jvalue — assigned EXACTLY once); reassignment (pattern re-instrumentation) is rare, so the cost moves from the hot read (every `*V` encounter) to the rare write. = GOT/PLT lazy binding / monomorphic inline cache with write-invalidation. **Scope: defer-of-VAR ONLY** (the lowerer's existing `TT_DEFER && c[0]->t==TT_VAR` split, ln 1626); deferred CALLS/EXPRS (`*LT(N,10)`, `*DIFFER(S)`) are genuine match-time predicates and keep the generic path.

**WHAT ALREADY EXISTS (verified in-tree s149 — do NOT rebuild):**
- **s142 DEFER-SITE DIET is LIVE on all three slow demos** (`g_sno_defer_cells` refs in emitted `.s`: calc-1-fence 14, calc-2-fence 22, treebank-fence 10). Fast path = cell load + test + `jmp rax`, γ/ω continuations register-passed in rcx/rdx (`bb_match_defer.cpp` ~ln 48-85). So the SITE-side bind is done — but WRITE-ONCE, licensed by the STATIC seal (`op_seal==2` + `g_gva_active` + `dw_cell()`), and `g_sno_fz_unsafe` (any EVAL/CODE/CLEAR) voids every static claim program-wide (lower ln 933).
- **The cells are ANONYMOUS** — emission-order indices, NO name→cell mapping exists. The runtime cannot invalidate them today. That registry is DB-1's concrete plumbing.
- **Callee PAT$ α anatomy (from calc-2-fence `.s`):** `sub rsp,96` + saves rcx(γ)/rdx(ω)/rbp + `mov rbp,rsp` + ~5 targeted zero-fills, per activation, millions of times = the heatmap's PAT$_α 38–61%. THE SITE IS DIETED; THE CALLEE α IS WHERE THE DEMO WALL LIVES.
- ZB-FC-4's "64KB donation" is NOT on the fast path per the s149 template read (slow-path `rt_defer_open`/`rt_proc_open_fn` side) — verify during DB-2, coordinate with that rung.

## ⭐⭐ PS LADDER — PATTERN-SIZE STAMP (mint s150, Lon design ruling: "suppose every DESCR with DT_P held the size / a lookup table of sizes were available"). SELF-DESCRIBING EXTENT DECOUPLES DB-3 FROM DB-1.
**THE RULING:** stamp every DT_P blob header with its per-activation ζ footprint at compile/freeze. ARBNO then latches the ARRIVING blob's stride at entry instead of needing a compile-time proof of which blob arrives — extent becomes data, not a compiler verdict. DB-3 no longer waits on DB-1's bind-stability; DB-1 shrinks to the call-target inline-cache role (read-path speed), still worth doing, no longer licensing.

(b) FZ-5b retired ⇒ every matched DT_P flows through `dtp_fn_of → bb_compile_pat_tree` = ONE stamping choke point; only the two eager builders (`bb_build_len_blob`/`bb_build_break_blob`) bypass it. (c) `DTP_t{fn,rcp}` header: `fn` pinned at offset 0 (bb_match_defer static assert) — fields APPEND. (d) `DESCR_t.slen` is 0/unused for DT_P — left 0; the header is the one home (survives NV copies by pointer share).

Lazy path stamps via `bb_compile_pat_tree_sz(tv,&zsz,&zstatic)` (old `bb_compile_pat_tree` now a wrapper); `bb_graph_zstatic()` walks `g->all[]` for DEFER/VALUE. Eager len/break stamp via `rt_gvar_assign_pat_sz`→`dtp_wrap_fn_sz`. SNO$MKPAT (the LIVE compiled-program path; `bb_ref_invariant` is effectively dead) stamps from proc registry via NEW `rt_fn_frame_bytes_known` (returns 0 not the 4096 default — soundness: a guessed size must never license arithmetic; caught + fixed the EVAL-PAT$ 4096-leak this session). Accessors `dtp_zsz_of`/`dtp_zstatic_of` for PS-3. `SCRIP_PSTAMP_TRACE=1`. **Verified: m3==m4 stamps identical; crosscheck watermark-exact both modes; `.s` deterministic + untouched.**

### DB-1 — DYNAMIC LICENSE: write-barrier fixup replaces the static seal gate
*Expectation set honestly: ~zero rail delta on the 5 demos (the diet is already active there). DB-1 is the CORRECTNESS/COVERAGE rung — it extends the diet to non-GVA/non-sealed/EVAL-containing programs and is what LICENSES DB-2/DB-3's per-site assumptions. Do not judge it by the rail.*
- [ ] DB-1a **Emit-time registry:** name → {cell indices}. Deferred-name set is compile-time static; emit a sealed table (name, cell idx list) + mark those names' NV entries with a flag bit (or a tiny static name list the barrier scans — the set is single-digit sized).
- [ ] DB-1b **Barrier:** (i) `NV_SET_fn` (the C choke point `bb_assign_global` calls) checks the flag → on hit, re-resolve: store new `dtp_fn_of(head)` if DT_P else 0 (=generic path) into that name's cells. (ii) Emitted fast path: LOWER marks assigns to deferred-set names; emitter plants the fixup only at those few sites — zero cost on all other assignments. (iii) `CLEAR()` resets all registered cells to 0.
- [ ] DB-1c **Drop `op_seal==2` + write-once from the cell gate** (keep the GVA arm first slice, then extend to the non-GVA NV arm). `g_sno_fz_unsafe` no longer voids the diet — EVAL/CODE programs keep it.
- [ ] DB-1d **Gates + new canaries:** crosscheck both modes plain+POISON at watermark; ADD two corpus canaries — (1) a program that REASSIGNS a deferred pattern variable mid-run (re-instrumentation), (2) one that assigns a STRING to a deferred name (non-DT_P fallback) — both byte-identical to sbl. Immediate-assignment-mid-match (`$ V` writing a deferred name) is correct by construction (a write is a write) — canary it anyway. ### DB-2 — CALLEE α DIET (the SPEED rung: PAT$_α 38–61% of the slow demos)

### s144 (2026-07-24, Claude) — BLOCKER C SOLVED: runtime CODE/EVAL fragments now emit+register PAT$N pattern procs · crosscheck improved 307/3→309/1 (m3), 302/6→304/4 (m4)

**ROOT CAUSE (proven by targeted trace, not guessed).** `sno_lower_fragment_at` calls `sno_pat_collect` during fragment lowering, which mints PAT$30+ into `g_sno_pats[]` (continuing past the main-program count of 30), but unlike `lower_sno_stage2` **never turned those entries into emitted+registered proc_table blobs**. At match time: `SNO$MKPAT("PAT$30")` → `rt_proc_get_fn` → NULL → 79 "not registered" errors (43 distinct PAT names, PAT$30–PAT$72; some referenced multiple times). Confirmed by `SNO_TRACE_PAT` instrumentation: 30 mint calls with `in_fragment=0` (main path, registered fine) vs 43 with `in_fragment=1` (fragment path, never registered). Trace removed before fix.

**CORRECTION to s143 cursor "79 distinct":** it's 79 error *lines* / 43 distinct PAT names (PAT$62/63/64 referenced 8× each). Recorded here for accuracy.

**THE FIX (SCRIP `880f2729`, 2 files):**
- `src/lower/lower_snobol4.c`: factored the main-path inline PAT-proc-build loop out of `lower_sno_stage2` into `sno_pat_thunks_build(int p0)` + `sno_pat_count()` getter. Main path now calls `sno_pat_thunks_build(0)` — behavior-identical. Both new symbols declared at call sites via `extern`; no header needed, no global namespace pollution. `$` is not a SNOBOL4 identifier character (emit.cpp:2276) so PAT$N names are proc-registry keys, never SNOBOL4 variable names.
- `src/runtime/runtime_eval.c`: in `code()` — capture `pat0 = sno_pat_count()` and `proc0 = g_stage2.proc_count` before lowering loop; after all fragment bodies emit, if new patterns were minted: `sno_pat_thunks_build(pat0)` (builds proc_table entries) then `eval_thunks_emit_from(proc0)` (emits+registers). Same hook added to `eval()` arm (latent version of same gap).

**RESULTS:**
- Beauty self-host PAT not-registered errors: **79 → 0**. stderr clean. rc=0, ~6s. beauty still prints `Parse Error` (Blocker D — see NEXT TARGET below).
- Crosscheck **IMPROVED with zero regressions** (verified by stashing fix + rebuilding pristine for true baseline): m3 307/3 → **309/1**, m4 302/6 → **304/4**. Fix incidentally repairs `140_pat_eval_double_fn_trick` + `141_pat_eval_double_fn_arbno` (both the PAT-EVAL corpus class). DIVERGE=3 (`214/215/216_indirect_goto*`) unchanged pre-existing m4 defect. `test_case` m3 fail pre-existing in pristine.
- Note: SPITBOL oracle *also* fails on `beauty.sno < beauty.sno` (rc=139 SIGSEGV, "duplicate label" syntax error) — self-formatting is not a clean byte-identity target for either engine. Blocker D diagnosis requires a 2-way monitor run (monitor-first per RULES).

**GATES (-O0):** sno smokes 7/7×2 · crosscheck m3 309/1 m4 304/4 DIVERGE=3 · beauty suite 48/51 ✅.
**SCRIP HEAD:** `880f2729` == origin/main. **RT_OPT=-O0.**

### s143 (2026-07-24, Claude) — BLOCKER B SOLVED: it was NOT FLAT_BUF_MAX; it was a proc_entry_node DRIVER OMISSION (1-line fix) · beauty now on Blocker C

**FULL WRITEUP:** `FINDING-2026-07-24-CLAUDE-SN4-BEAUTY-BLOCKER-B-IS-PROC-ENTRY-NODE-DRIVER-OMISSION-NOT-FLAT-BUF-MAX.md`.

**⛔ CORRECTION to s142's Blocker B (below): the "FLAT_BUF_MAX exceeded" headline was WRONG.** beauty's main graph is ~217 KB, not >1 MB — the overflow guard (emit.cpp:2326, the ONLY path that prints the FLAT_BUF_MAX line) never fires. The generic `emit_chain returned NULL` fatal came from the OTHER, SILENT NULL path (`bb_alloc` fail, emit.cpp:2320). Instrumented + proved: beauty's 314 `LBL__` procs ALL share main's `bb_idx=0`, and the mode-3 driver proc-emit loop **`scrip.c:1358` was passing `s2->bbp.table[idx]->entry` (main's TRUE entry), NOT `proc_entry_node`** — so it re-emitted + re-sealed main's WHOLE ~217 KB graph 314 times. Pool (16 MB) exhausts at ~73 procs (`pool_used`=15,847,424), then every later `emit_chain` returns NULL. s142's own suggested "bb_idx-seen guard + rt_proc_set_fn at anchor offset" was the heavy alternative; the ACTUAL fix is 1 line.

**THE FIX (landed, `src/driver/scrip.c:1358`, 1 line):** `emit_chain(s2->bbp.table[idx]->entry, …)` → `emit_chain(bb_proc_entry(&s2->proc_table[_pi]), …)`. Root cause: s142 set `proc_entry_node` on LBL__ procs and updated TWO of the THREE driver loops (1177 m4 ✓, 1458 m3 ✓) but MISSED this third one (1358, the path beauty takes). `bb_proc_entry` (gen_runtime.h:50) returns `proc_entry_node` if set else `g->entry` — so ordinary DEFINE procs (proc_entry_node==NULL) still emit from their own entry (raw `.proc_entry_node` would pass NULL → early-return NULL → unregistered proc; the accessor's fallback is why it's safer than the sibling idiom). Non-CODE programs are byte-inert.

**RESULT — beauty advanced past Blocker B onto Blocker C:**
- 2-way monitor (`PARTICIPANTS="spl scr"`): divergence moved **step 1 → step 2** (RULES monitor criterion met — moved PAST old site).
- Direct `--run`: rc=0, ~8 s (was 28 s → SIGABRT). Lands on **79 distinct `SNO$MKPAT: PAT$NN not registered` starting at PAT$30** = Blocker C exactly as forecast.

**⭐ BONUS — the fix RESTORES the cursor's own claimed crosscheck watermark.** Pristine HEAD `21cfe7aa` MEASURES **m3 302/8** (matches line 11 below; the 307/3 quoted elsewhere in-cursor was stale). The fix → **m3 307/3**, fixing 5 CODE/indirect-goto programs: `1020_code_label_transfer`, `1021_code_direct_goto`, `214/215/216_indirect_goto*`. Pristine `->entry` re-emission produced WRONG mode-3 results for all five.

### s142 (2026-07-24, Claude) — LBL__ O(n²) ZLS OVERFLOW KILLED · beauty monitor: ZLS cleared, FLAT_BUF_MAX next

**BEAUTY TEST SUITE: 48/51 ✅** (watermark-exact; 3 omega_driver pre-existing).

**BEAUTY SELF-HOST monitor run (PARTICIPANTS="spl scr", STDIN_SRC=beauty.sno):**
- **Blocker A CLEARED (SCRIP `a5d985ba`):** 2-way monitor confirmed `zls: entry table overflow (65536)` at step 1. Root cause: beauty's indirect gotos (`:(VAR)` dispatch) set `g_sno_uses_code=1`, firing the LBL__ loop that called `sno_build_graph` once per label — 163 labels × full stmt re-lowering = O(n²) ZLS marks → table overflow before any emission.  **Fix:** LBL__ loop replaced with O(n) shared-graph design: each label becomes a `LBL__<name>` proc sharing main's `bb_idx` with `proc_entry_node = bb_label_landing(lbl)` (already in main's registry after main build). Zero extra graphs, zero extra ZLS marks. Infrastructure already live: `bb_proc_entry()` fallback in gen_runtime.h, Pascal lowerer identical pattern. `SCRIP_SN4_STUB` gate removed from LBL__ arm (orthogonal). `emit_chain` NULL now prints: overflow flag, nbytes, pos, cap, patch counts.
- **Blocker B (NEXT):** After ZLS fix, beauty compiles its full graph (~30s) then hits **`emit_chain('pat_flat') FAILED: FLAT_BUF_MAX exceeded (graph too large for 1MB flat buffer)`** — beauty's main graph is larger than the 1MB `FLAT_BUF_MAX`. Options: (a) raise `FLAT_BUF_MAX` (trivial band-aid, possibly sufficient — beauty is a compiler, biggest sno program in corpus); (b) split the emit into per-proc chunks; (c) check whether LBL__ procs sharing main's bb_idx causes the driver to re-emit main's full graph N=163 times (most likely!). **Diagnose the re-emission first** — the driver loop at scrip.c iterates ALL procs by bb_idx; LBL__ procs share main's bb_idx so the driver may be calling `emit_chain(proc_entry_node, ...)` 163 times starting from different anchors inside the same 1MB budget. If so: add a `bb_idx`-seen guard in the driver so shared-graph procs skip the jmp-entry emission and instead register with `rt_proc_set_fn` pointing to the already-emitted blob at the anchor offset (the address of `anchor` within the sealed buf — `(char*)buf + anchor->offset`). That is the correct O(n) LBL__ runtime registration.
- **ROADMAP:** after Blocker B: Blocker C = runtime PAT$30+ blob registration (CODE()-compiled patterns not found by `SNO$MKPAT`). Monitor drives each fix: re-run monitor after B, fix first new divergence.

# GOAL — SNOBOL4 on the shared BB spine

## ⛔⛔ O2-DIRECTED-ONLY — TIGHTENS O0-DEV BELOW (Lon directive, 2026-07-22 s126)

**`-O2` (and `-O1`) may not be used for ANYTHING — not even perf/benchmark measurement — unless Lon explicitly directs it in the current session.** This SUPERSEDES the "reserved EXCLUSIVELY for perf" carve-out in the O0-DEV FACT RULE below (that body is md5-locked across the six GOAL-*-BB files and is left untouched; THIS block is the SNOBOL4-side tightening — see RULES.md for the repo-wide rule). WHY: the `-O2` runtime `.so` build takes minutes, which collides with the assistant's per-tool-call time limits; the only workaround (detached `setsid` background build + polling) was tried s126 and is FRAGILE — a detached job died mid-clone and left a poisoned half-tree that a later "idempotent" script tripped over (`build_official_oracles.sh`'s `[ -d .git ]` guard skipped re-cloning an empty worktree). CONSEQUENCE FOR BENCHMARKS: SCRIP perf numbers are `-O0`-runtime numbers unless a session log shows Lon directing `-O2`; label every reported number with its RT_OPT level so slower-than-oracle results are not misread (the officials build optimized). If a directed `-O2` build happens: detached + polled, with an explicit tree-state check before AND after (the s126 poisoned-clone lesson).

---

## ⛔ FACT RULE — O0-DEV: FEATURE BUILDS ARE `-O0`; `-O1`/`-O2` ARE PERF-ONLY (Lon directive, 2026-07-21 s119)

**While developing, debugging, or iterating on any FEATURE, EVERY build is `-O0`. `-O1` and `-O2` are FORBIDDEN during feature work and are reserved EXCLUSIVELY for perf/benchmark/release measurement.** The runtime `libscrip_rt.so` at `-O2` takes MINUTES (heavy template TUs), which is intolerable in a compile→test→fix loop and burned real session time repeatedly. `scrip` itself already builds `-O0` (Makefile `CBASE`/`CXXRT`); the offender was the runtime `.so`, whose `RT_OPT` default was `-O2`.

**THE MECHANICAL ANCHOR (why this is a FACT RULE, not a convention):** the Makefile default is now `RT_OPT ?= -O0 …` (SCRIP `Makefile` lines ~33 + ~281), so a bare `make libscrip_rt` / `make scrip` / `build_scrip.sh` is `-O0` by DEFAULT — the fast path is the path you get for free. `-O2` is now EXPLICIT opt-in, used ONLY for measurement:
```
make RT_OPT="-O2 -g -fno-strict-aliasing -fwrapv -fno-omit-frame-pointer" libscrip_rt   # perf/bench ONLY
PERF=1 bash scripts/jcon_selfhost_build.sh                                               # perf .so via the selfhost builder
```
Benchmark builders that need `-O2` already pass it explicitly (`jcon_selfhost_build.sh PERF=1`; the official-oracle trees build their own way), so the default flip does NOT silently corrupt any perf number — a perf run that forgets `-O2` is a mis-measurement the operator owns, not a default that lies.

**COMPLETION TEST:** (a) `grep -nE 'RT_OPT *[?:]?= *-O0' Makefile` matches (default is `-O0`) and no un-opted `RT_OPT ?= -O2` remains; (b) session-setup / feature-dev build scripts (`build_scrip.sh`, smoke/crosscheck runners) invoke `make` with NO `RT_OPT` override (so they inherit `-O0`); (c) any `-O2` in a script is either a monitor/oracle-side helper (separate lib) or gated behind an explicit perf flag (`PERF=1`); (d) this FACT RULE body is byte-identical across the six GOAL-*-BB files (md5-locked, per the Prolog file's sibling-verbatim note).

**LIMITATION (do not oversell — same honest shape as the other rules here):** a Makefile default and a markdown rule cannot COERCE a session to avoid typing `RT_OPT=-O2` during feature work; they make the fast path the default and the slow path a deliberate, visible choice. The human reviewer remains the real enforcer — **reject any feature-work handoff whose build log shows `-O2` on the runtime `.so`.**

Full 3-way results: **`SCRIP/README.md` § "SNOBOL4 Benchmark"** (all 16 byte-identical across SPITBOL `4fe74db` / CSNOBOL4 `bd51fcb5` / SCRIP m4; scoreboard updated s95: **9/15 vs CSNOBOL4, 5/15 vs SPITBOL** — func_call + func_call_overhead newly won via BP-1 dispatch removal). The emitted code WINS where it is register-shaped (var_access 0.17×, op_dispatch 0.23×, arith_loop 0.35× vs SPITBOL); remaining losses are RUNTIME-LIBRARY-shaped — string building/churn, pattern scanner on string-heavy input, and EVAL.

**GATES for every BP slice:** clean build (scrip + libscrip_rt) · sno smokes 7/7×2 · crosscheck ≥ watermark m3 307/0 · m4 307/0 · DIVERGE=0 · bench harness ≥15/16 OK with 3-way output identity intact · before/after self-ms for targeted benchmarks recorded here. BP-1..4 + BP-6 are RUNTIME-ONLY (no template/encoder edits — no BB-CODEGEN doc gate). BP-5/BP-6 touch gc_heap.c (read s89–s92 findings first). BP-7 is BB-CODEGEN (ARCH-ICON.md + GOAL-TEMPLATE-REVAMP-RULES-DRAFT.md MANDATORY first).

- [ ] **BP-7 SLOT-BASED CALL CONVENTION** — func_call ~103ns/call spread across rt_proc_call_prologue / rt_name_save_push / rt_proc_find / restore: dynamic scope paid through name-table ops per call. Emit-time proc resolution + frame-offset save/restore. **BB-CODEGEN GATED** — design-set docs first. Targets: func_call 2.4×, func_call_overhead 2.3×, fibonacci 3.1×, roman tail. **s97 SCOPING (profile + manual):** gdb samples spread across save_push/prologue/epilogue + emitted code; rt_proc_find ALREADY ptr-memoized (g_proc_idx_key) and save_push already consumes precomputed cells[] — NO runtime-only snipe remains, the cost IS the per-call C transfer + save loop + dynamic-scope memory traffic. Manual semantics to preserve (DEFINE): save-set = the prototype's formals+locals, "saved prior to function entry, and restored upon function return"; the fname variable is the return channel; RETURN/NRETURN/FRETURN labels (those wires already split by s62/s63 PROC-CONV). DESIGN SHAPE: keep each NAME's global cell as the single storage location (dynamic scope stays intact for recursive/indirect readers); at α emit inline movs old-cell→caller-frame-slot + arg→cell, reverse at γ/ω — g_name_save stack + rt_name_save_push/restore C calls disappear for STATICALLY-DEFINED procs (literal prototype in label-free init, the common case, resolvable at emit from the existing DEFINE lowering); computed/re-DEFINE keeps the by-name C path as runtime fallback (OLD/NEW switch shape — the ARCH-ICON variable-model precedent).
- [ ] **BP-8 (LAST OR NOT AT ALL — Lon 2026-07-18)** eval_dynamic per-distinct-EVAL full pipeline ~76µs (~57µs emit; probed s94, probe reverted). Only after BP-1..7.
- [ ] **BP-9 BRANCH-CHAIN ACROSS ζ-POPS (Lon 2026-07-18 s98, from Proebsting §5; does NOT wait on BP-8).** Proebsting names the naive four-port expansion's disease — "many simple copies and many branches to branches" — and the cure: copy propagation + branch chaining + code reordering. SCRIP state MEASURED s98: the IR-level pass EXISTS (`src/optimizer/branch_chain.c`, chases `IR_SUCCEED`/`IR_GOTO` passthroughs, monitor-stamped gotos protected) but the EMITTED seams still trampoline — func_call.s carries 41 jmp→label→jmp chains of 93 total jmps. Under RSP-ζ self-allocation the ω/unwind trampolines are NOT bare jmps — each is `add rsp, op_sb_k` (the box's ζ pop) `; jmp next` — so the leap-frog collapse must ACCUMULATE the pops: every op_sb is emit-time static, so a chain folds to ONE `add rsp, Σ op_sb_k; jmp final` — the same branch-chain logic plus the adding of the known BB ζ sizes in-between the two ends of the leap-frog jump (Lon's formulation, recorded verbatim). Steps: **(1) BC-CENSUS** — greppable script over the 16-bench .s corpus counting (a) bare jmp→jmp chains, (b) add-rsp+jmp chains, per program; classify which wires (β/ω/port glue) carry them. **(2) BC-IR** — verify branch_chain.c fires for sno_bb BOTH modes; extend the passthrough set to any wire arm the census shows it stops at (respect the monitor-stamp protection — stamped gotos stay). **(3) BC-EMIT-ACC** — in the emit driver's wire/label resolution, when a jump target's chunk is exactly [`add rsp, N`]* `jmp L2`, fold ΣN into the jumping site's own adjustment and retarget L2; iterate to fixpoint; any label with an external entry (port, computed goto, pair-loop driver-minted label) is a chain BARRIER — never folded through. **(4) BC-GATE** — byte-identity on programs the census shows chain-free, crosscheck ≥ watermark ×2 modes, monitor 2-way spot-run (stamp protection proven live), bench A/B recorded here (targets: func_call/fibonacci tails, pattern_bt, roman). Monitor-first on any divergence.

## ⭐⭐⭐ RUNG SPD — SPITBOL DOMINANCE: SNOBOL4 3–4× FASTER THAN SPITBOL (Lon 2026-07-19 s108: "SPITBOL is threaded code, SCRIP is a true compiler — we should be 3-4 times faster. Identify the bottleneck empirically.")

**TARGETS (s108 container sbl medians):** claws5 ≤ ~12ms (now 412–530; sbl 38) · treebank ≤ ~45ms (now ~900; sbl ~165). Headroom ≈ 40×; rungs sequenced by MEASURED share.

**EVIDENCE OF RECORD (s108; tools: callgrind --smc-check [counts runtime-emitted blobs], ASLR-pinned loop-exec PC sampling, live blob disassembly — no perf counters in container):** claws5 m4 = 1.31B Ir: **pattern blob 58.7% (771M)** · by-name complex 10.5% (strcmp 6.9 + try_call 3.0 + script_try 0.6) · alloc/copy ~9% (memcpy 2.6, sxt_extend 2.0, memset 1.4, gcheap_alloc 1.4, concat 1.0, carve 1.0) · pattern_match.c:sort_fn 2.9 (⚠ audit) · subscript/tbl ~2.5. Wall sampling at 1× workload: ~85% in-blob, one hot window = nested N=2/3 ALTs whose **arms do an indirect C call (libc memcmp-family) per elementary match attempt**: `movslq r14d,rcx; lea (r13,rcx),rdi; movabs lit,rsi; movabs len,rdx; movabs fn,rax; call *rax; test; jne` ≈ 15+ insts + call overhead where SPITBOL inlines a compare. Also seen: zero-imm placeholder `add/sub $0` live in hot path; δ round-trips through [rbp+off] at every seam. SPITBOL white-box (nm+gdb, non-PIE): threaded dispatch = 4 insts (`mov (%r13),%r10; mov %r10,%rdi; add $8,%r13; jmp *(%rdi)`); claws5 under sbl is **concat-bound** (modal = `rep movsb` in o_cnc; alocs + two block copies) — its scanning never samples. ⚠ 150×-input sampling shows the ASYMPTOTIC régime (memcpy+gc_cons_scan_t dominate) — a DIFFERENT profile than 1×; always sample the true workload via loop-exec.

- [ ] **SPD-0 ATTRIBUTION HARNESS.** (a) commit scripts/profile_callgrind.sh (blob-vs-C split table) + scripts/profile_pcsample.sh (loop-exec sampler; fix sbl-side pgrep-through-setarch). (b) SCRIP_BLOB_MAP=1 in emit_chain: label→address dump so blob hotspots name their TEMPLATE. (c) both programs × both modes tables here; name every call-*rax target. Gate: scripts green, tables recorded.
- [ ] **SPD-1 INLINE THE ELEMENTARY TESTS (the 59%).** Short literals (len ≤ 8) → inline masked qword compare / len∈{1,2,4} width compares against baked immediates (16B DT_S tail pad per BP-6 makes the 8B over-read safe — VERIFY carve pad invariant first); 1-char → cmpb. Variable-arm ANY/NOTANY/SPAN/BREAK → inline the s106 256-byte-table loop (today only frozen-LITERAL arms have it; variable arms still call strchr). Delete zero-imm placeholder add/sub at emission when patched value is 0. Gates per slice: watermark m3 307/0 · m4 307/0 · DIVERGE=0 · demo_full_3way PASS · **interleaved lib-swap A/B ×7 medians (the ONLY accepted perf evidence — s108 bimodality)** · gc STRESS suite for the over-read slice.
- [ ] **SPD-2 SEAM-TRAFFIC DIET.** δ lives in r14d (ARCH-ICON contract) yet bounces through FR at every α/β/advance; audit protocol-required saves (choice points) vs habit; emit saves only at choice points. Measure via SPD-0b template attribution first.
- [ ] **SPD-3 FUSED SCANNER SUPEROPS.** Freeze-time peephole in bb_compile_pat_tree: literal-concat runs → one compare; SPAN/BREAK(cset) + literal → one loop + trailing compare; constant POS/RPOS/LEN chains → single bounds arithmetic. IR-kind dispatch only (FACT rules). Gate adds: monitor spitbol_vs_run pattern suite.
- [ ] **SPD-4 BY-NAME RESIDUE (10.5%).** Bind strcmp 6.9% to callers (callgrind tree); emit-time resolution for statically-known builtin names (BP-1 v2 recipe); intern names → pointer compare.
- [ ] **SPD-5 ALLOC/COPY + ODDITIES.** Audit sort_fn 2.9% (if not the SORT builtin, kill); CAPTURE-INTO-SLOT (s107 queue); treebank n-ary-concat single-guard-scan; gc_blk_of/pin walk (treebank ~14%).
- [ ] **SPD-6 TREEBANK OWN TABLE.** Different régime (5.6×; asymptote = memcpy + gc scan). SPD-0 tables, then its top-3 as sub-steps.
- [ ] **SPD-7 SEAM DENSITY (BP-9 finish, LAST).** Chain-collapse w/ Σop_sb; expect density not speed; after the Ir cuts so I-cache effects are measurable.

**METHOD:** monitor-first on divergence · rebuild scrip AND libscrip_rt before judging template changes (s106 trap) · one rung per commit, gates green · interleaved A/B only.

| **EVAL/CODE = DEFER SHAPE (Lon ruling s59, 2026-07-14)** | **EVAL is the SAME type of thing as DEFER: call a function to RESOLVE (lookup/compile — a C call is fine, it is not the transfer), then WIRE the outside γ/ω and JUMP to the BB; the BB jumps back through the wires. CODE is the same, just deferred EXECUTION instead of deferred evaluation. When NCB-3b retires the `rt_eval_run` C shim, the INVOKE converts to this exact protocol — resolve → wire → jmp / jmp-back, self-allocating activation, no call/ret in the transfer. The ZS-2 DEFER conversion is the reference implementation.** |

## ⭐⭐ RUNG SAVE-FRONTIER — SPECIAL BB + OPERAND LINKAGE + PINNED ISLAND (Lon experiment s74 2026-07-16; pre-REG-MAP probe for op_anchored DEFER window class — walk ×4: 117/142/165/070)
**⛔ PARKED s75 (Lon ruling; see s75 LAST SESSION §1–2): construction skipped — measurement banked (`c06f6ccf`), REG-3 subsumes the class, saved-RSP home = RBP + wire-header [+24] on-frame. SF-0..4 below are NOT to be built; prose retained as the empirical record.**

**EMPIRICAL BASIS (slice 0, SCRIP `c06f6ccf`):** saved-RSP − RSP at DEFER's β seam recovers the unknown-children footprint byte-exactly. Measured: pa 176B · pc 192B (+16/ALT cell, live frontier not constant) · pd nested 352B (nesting correct through one diff) · callout s2−g6=0 · corpus 117 γ-retained 176B/element. All oracle-exact while gated. LIFO law holds empirically. Diagnostic is additive-only (env-gated `SCRIP_RSPDIFF=1`, zero bytes changed gate-off).

**DESIGN (Lon "special BB" → correct IR structure):** mint `IR_SAVE_FRONTIER` BB wired at window entry; DEFER + window flat-reading boxes operand-linked to it. Same fc registrar mechanism as COND/SAVE-cell admission (COND.operands[0]==R law) — language-blind, per IR kind, finalize resolves disps. Slot lives on a small PINNED ISLAND (absolute-disp32 baked at emit, identical both mediums — MODE34 law; rides RT_PIN_BASE / RT_CAS_TOP precedent from REG-0). Protocol: α pushes frontier onto island stack; ω/exhaust pops; β reads island top absolutely — no frame-base register needed, circularity broken. Nesting correct by LIFO (nested probe measured; deepest ω pops before outer β). ⛔ SEAL-POP NOTE: FENCE-sealed boxes skip ω arms; seal glue must pop skipped island entries (same obligation as FPB-pop at FENCE-seal L2, s71 lesson).

## ⭐⭐ RUNG GC-U — UNIFIED GC: THE s60 PIVOT (Lon directive 2026-07-14, live session)

**THE DIRECTIVE (recorded):** implement a unified GC handling MARK/ADJUST/SLIDE, the CSNOBOL4/SIL shape. The GVA is its own mmap section. SNOBOL4 gets a CONDITIONAL ASSIGN stack in a separate mmap. The collector scans the live ζ blocks, the GVA, and all other SPECIAL mmap structs for references → MARK, then ADJUST, then SLIDE. RSP-ζ is ON HOLD: ONE GC arena + several SPECIAL stacks for now; when ζ lands on rsp, marking becomes a straight BB traversal back down the stack (up in memory).

**RECONCILIATION — the directive lands on the EXISTING Phase 0, which is ~40% built. Nothing re-invented: three rulings recorded, two new steps, one verification. All prior rung IDs (TR-4/TR-5/GC-W-0..3/CS-1/2/TR-6..8) unchanged.**

### s60 RULINGS OF RECORD
- **R1 — SUPERSEDED s60b (misread):** ZS was never on hold. See the s60b CORRECTIONS below — ZS finishes FIRST, then this ladder.
- **R2 — THE GC-W-1 ROOT-SET FORK IS RULED; the s40 landmine closes.** Roots = **live ζ blocks + GVA island + the special mmap structs** (CAS stack per CAS-1 below, dictionary bucket statics, ledgered C statics) — scanned for references, then MARKed, then ADJUSTed. INTERIM meaning of "live ζ" (ζ on the zeta arena while RSP is on hold): **on-chain ≡ live = fork (b)** — the chain is BOTH the root enumeration AND a discipline: frames are explicitly unlinked at generator exhaust / cut / determinate exit (= **ZLS-LEAK**, promoted to the ladder's first step; measured a PROLOG-only problem — SNOBOL4 517/517 LIFO, Icon 20486/20488, tak.pl's 63,609 abandoned frames the exhibit, s40). END-STATE meaning (post-FLIP): **on-rsp ≡ live** — marking is the BB traversal back down the stack (up in memory) via the walkable ζ frame chain the α/ω protocol maintains (Region 1 obligation, already written at the region banner). ⇒ **The ζ root scanner is an INTERFACE with two flavors:** v1 = arena chain-walk (this ladder), v2 = rsp frame-walk (lands only when ZS resumes; frame maps derive from the ZK kind cells + flat_frame_bytes). GC-W-1/GC-W-2 MUST NOT bake v1 in.
- **R3 — SIL SHAPE CONFIRMED (nothing new to derive).** MARK (trace from roots, set title-word mark) → ADJUST (linear sweep computes slid-down addresses; per-block-type relocatable-word maps rewrite every reference in marked blocks AND roots) → SLIDE (order-preserving memmove, free pointer resets; bump alloc between collections). Design + manual pins already extracted at **ARCH-ZETA §6** — READ IT before GC-W-2 (COLLECT(i) contract manual p.216; DESCR tags replace the SIL magnitude trick: no &MAXLNGTH cap, no address floor).

### ⭐ s60b CORRECTIONS (Lon, live session — these govern; they supersede R1 and amend the block)
- **C1 — ZS IS NOT ON HOLD; IT FINISHES FIRST.** Remaining ZS work: PROC-CONV → EVAL/CODE (the DEFER jmp-entry shape — Lon: "the remaining last work") → SLOT-MIGRATE → FLIP. The RESUME ORDER below is **BLOCKED on ZS completion**. Consequence: SNOBOL4 arrives at GC-W-1 via scanner **v2 (rsp BB traversal) as PRIMARY**; the v1 chain-walk remains only for languages whose ζ is still arena-resident.
- **C2 — BBs NEED ζ ALLOCATION, NOT STACKS (design law of record).** A co-expression does not need "a stack"; its BBs need ζ ALLOCATION, and the allocation source is controlled at the PORT HOOKS (α/β/γ/ω) — RSP-based or otherwise. Generalizes the s32 two-flavor law: **TWO TEMPLATE MODES — RSP-ZETA and HEAP-ZETA — selected at the port layer; templates flavor-blind.**
- **C3 — HEAP-ζ REGION (escapee BBs), CONFIRMED.** Escapee-BB ζ goes to its OWN mmap region (the promoted ZH, zeta_heap.c — s37's "delete ZH machinery at GC-W" is REVERSED): the collector SCANS it as a root area and ADJUSTs its DESCRs; never collected, never slid; **reclamation = the region's own LIVE/DEAD lifecycle (Lon: yes).** HEAP-ZETA port mode allocates here. Step HZ-1 added below.
- **C4 — CO-EXPRESSIONS DEFERRED.** No coswitch rung now; the pthread baton stays load-bearing (GC-W-0's s49 verification stands). Under C2 a coexpr's BBs are simply HEAP-ZETA-mode citizens when we get there.
- **C5 — ZLS-LEAK DEMOTED** (consequence of scan-only ζ regions): a chained dead frame can over-RETAIN, never corrupt ⇒ an RSS fix parked beside the ladder, NOT a GC precondition.

### NEW STEPS (the only genuinely new work)
- **CAS-1 — CONDITIONAL-ASSIGN STACK → OWN MMAP ISLAND.** The conditional-assign/capture machinery — `rt_zcol_push` (pattern_match.c:1143, today ZC_COL_MALLOC/ZC_COL_ARENA realloc-style growth behind the `ZC_COLLECTION` switch) plus the dcap growth tables (`g_dcf`/`g_capx`/`g_dfx`, pattern_match.c ~:611/:728/:820) — moves to a dedicated base-pinned mmap island in `rt_gva_island`'s class: **root-scanned, an ADJUST client, never slides, never collected.** CAS-1 opens with a CENSUS deciding which of the four tables constitute THE stack (all four are match-lifetime DESCR/view-bearing growth tables today). The ZC_COLLECTION switch is the seam — add the island flavor beside the existing arms, keep the A/B. Manual grounding: conditional (`.`) assignment defers to overall match success (the deferred-`*PUSH()` passage) — the stack holds pending {prev_view, saved_δ} + capture windows DURING the match ⇒ DESCR-bearing ⇒ root + adjust client, exactly like the GVA. Base-pinned ⇒ emitted code MAY bake its addresses (same license as GVA under D4). Gate: crosscheck byte-identical both modes; island bounds-asserted; smokes ×2.

### ⭐ THE ZS LADDER — LIVE (s60b), resume here: the s59 PROC-CONV spec, verbatim
**PROC-CONV — R12-FREE ladder rung 2 (Lon s58). Convert the proc transfer to the jmp-entry protocol ZS-2 landed for pattern blobs: rt.c:549/878/892/980 + :591/:608 family. ### ⛔ WHY THIS BLOCK EXISTS — the staleness was never a git problem (s47 diagnosis)
Every session's "stale info at startup" has ONE root cause, and it is **not** `perform hand off`, not the credential, not the clone: **the repos are always correct** (s47 verified: SCRIP/corpus/`.github` all at origin, nothing behind). What rots is **prose that asserts things it cannot know**:
1. **`PUSH PENDING` banners are structurally unable to be true.** They claim the status of an event that happens AFTER the text is frozen into the commit. Eleven of them said "HANDOFF BLOCKED" while the work sat safely on origin. **RULE: never write push status into a doc. `scripts/handoff_status.sh` is the ONLY ground truth, and it is run live.** (All 11 voided s47.)
2. **The WORK ORDER banner was abandoned, not maintained.** Nothing in the handoff sequence ever obliged anyone to record *where the work actually moved to*. Hence this block. **RULE: the handoff updates NEXT RUNG here, or the handoff is not done.**
3. **SESSION STATE blocks accreted out of chronological order** (s43 before s47 before s46 … s39 buried after s27), so "what happened last" required reading all 19 and comparing dates. **RULE: newest goes at the TOP of the session-state stack; prune below the last ~3.**
⚠ Note for concurrent sessions: 3–4 chats run in parallel on different GOAL files. That is safe for *code* (different files, git rebases cleanly) but means **`PLAN.md`'s per-goal Step column is stale by design** (RULES step 3 forbids editing it on routine handoff). **Trust THIS block, not PLAN.md's table.**

## ⛔⛔ THE WORK ORDER — top to bottom, first `- [ ]` wins (Lon directive s22: "work this whole thing in the correct order")

## ⛔⛔⛔ PHASE 0 — RUNG TR — THREE-REGION MEMORY REWRITE (s35 opened as TWO-REGION, Lon: "All sessions will wait for our rewrite."; s36 taxonomy ruling landed as the slab-pool/arena kernel; **AMENDED s37 2026-07-12, Lon ratification: "we are now proceeding with the three region memory scheme"** — the code slab is NAMED a region instead of hiding as 'unmanaged program image', and Region 2's reclaim story is RULED: SPITBOL-style stop-the-world MARK→FORWARD→ADJUST→SLIDE compaction — NOT grow-only-immortal arenas, NOT refcount) — **THE WORK-ORDER HEAD; EVERY PHASE BELOW WAITS**

**THE DESIGN RULE OF RECORD (ratified s37): all memory lives in exactly THREE regions — one collector, one mark phase, two outcomes: data slides, code free-lists.** History: s35's two-region banner + A-PROG/A-TRANS/A-SUSP/A-COEXPR arena taxonomy is SUPERSEDED on the heap side (full text in git history; the six-arena census that motivated it stands: rsp ζ · ZH · ZLS · libgc · GC-0 · raw malloc). What SURVIVES of s36's landings: `rt_slab.c`'s ONE-malloc-caller pool + `rt_slab_region` contiguous spans (they ARE the workspace's backing) + title words (PROMOTED from insurance to load-bearing: type|size|mark; forwarding layout ruled at GC-W-2) + the ζ-block reuse ruling (rehomed as ordinary collected objects at GC-W). THE TRIGGER for the amendment (s37 conversation of record): A-PROG grow-only-immortal never answered global-reachable CHURN — `S = S CH` loops and scratch TABLEs orphan every prior payload into an arena that never shrinks, and string-building into globals is the language's core workload (beauty, claws5, treebank); the s35 hedge "collector written later or never" was the tell — "never" was optimistic.

**REGION 1 — C STACK (rsp).** Forth BB cells (ARCH-ZETA §10), C runtime call frames, proc frames, every control-flow-lifetime ζ — unchanged, PLUS the ONE new stack-side obligation: a WALKABLE ζ FRAME CHAIN maintained by the α/ω protocol so the root scan can (a) enumerate stack-held DESCRs and (b) range-check saved return addresses against Region 3 (marks live code fragments for CS-2).

**REGION 2 — GC'D DATA WORKSPACE.** ONE contiguous reserved-VA span (reserve GBs PROT_NONE, commit on demand; base NEVER moves ⇒ `in-range ⇒ movable data object` is two compares — the 64-bit form of the manual's MXLEN-below-workspace magnitude trick). Alloc = bump cursor + title word. Reclaim = STW compaction on cursor exhaustion, pass order MARK→FORWARD→ADJUST→SLIDE (adjust BEFORE the move — the old copies are what you walk); vacated tail re-zeroed post-slide; grow = commit pages when post-slide free fraction < threshold (manual: SPITBOL requests additional memory from the OS when working storage is full). Everything dynamic that is not stack lives HERE: escapee/suspended ζ (ordinary collected objects reachable from generator/coexpr handles — ZH's LIVE/DEAD+lazy-retreat machinery DELETED at GC-W; the collector IS that mechanism done properly), the variable dictionary (bucket array = a root set; SPITBOL's drop-null-unreferenced-variables nicety optional, GC-W-3), globals payloads, stored patterns, TABLE/ARRAY storage, NV nodes, DT_S payloads, EVAL/CODE temp chains. **REFCOUNT RULED OUT (s37, reasons of record):** (1) inc/dec on escaping stores lands in EMITTED code = template-sweep-class cost (the 4989-site lesson); (2) the slide half already requires full pointer enumeration = the tracer, so RC adds only promptness; (3) the manual is blunt that promptness is an anti-goal ("forcing garbage collections before they are necessary will always increase execution time"); (4) cycles (`A<1> = A`, DATA fields, self-holding TABLEs) leak under pure RC — Python needed a backup tracer. (Fact worth keeping: `*P` deferral stores the NAME, so recursive patterns are NOT pointer cycles.)

**REGION 3 — CODE SLAB.** Program image that GROWS (CODE()/EVAL() fragments) — a region by honest name. DUAL-MAPPED once at startup (memfd_create: one RW view for the emitter, one RX view for execution, constant delta; protections NEVER change again ⇒ no mprotect churn, no TLB shootdowns, no coexpr-thread races, W^X-clean; plain RWX = the lazy fallback on stock Linux). **CODE NEVER MOVES** — native return addresses live on C stacks; DELIBERATE DIVERGENCE from SPITBOL, which keeps code/expression blocks inside its collected workspace. Fragment header = size+mark; fragment table bump-ordered ⇒ saved-RIP interior pointers resolve by binary search. V1 = grow-only bump; reclaim = CS-2 free list (address-ordered FIRST FIT + boundary-tag immediate coalescing — Johnstone&Wilson: policy ≈ noise once you coalesce; ~32B no-split threshold). `pat_pool.c`'s 4MB RWX pattern-blob pool = a Region-3 citizen (TR-0 sanction stands; unify under CS-1 or ledger it).

  - [ ] **GC-W-0 — BATON VERIFY + WORKSPACE CONSOLIDATION.** ✅ **BATON HALF VERIFIED s49 (measured in rt_coexpr.c, not assumed):** every coexpr semaphore inits to 0; a fresh thread's FIRST act in the trampoline is `sem_wait` (zero mutator code before first activation); `scrip_coswitch` = `sem_post(new); sem_wait(old)` — the post→wait window contains ONLY the switch's own synchronization instructions, so **exactly one thread is ever inside mutator/allocator code**; `scrip_coret` writes only its OWN ctx xmit slots; destroy = post+join with the dying thread exiting on !alive. STW collection therefore needs zero thread rendezvous (exhaustion triggers live at allocation sites, which are single-threaded by this discipline). NO STOP, NO RE-RULE. Remaining half: collapse the TR-2 spans (ZLS/ZLS2/GC-0 backings) + all TR-3 destinations into ONE reserved-VA workspace, ONE cursor, uniform title words (type|size|mark). Gate: watermark-identical; the in-range test = two compares against one [base,end).

PROOF: two forced `GC_gcollect()` on `tak.pl` reclaim **0.2MB of a 500MB heap** (fib/nrev: exactly ZERO) — libgc collects NOTHING from this family today, because the chain holds it all. ⇒ **If the chain is the root set, GC-W-2 can never collect the exact class it exists to collect** (abandoned generator frames = 63,609 of them, 250MB, on tak alone). The chain CANNOT be both the root set AND the holder of dead frames. **LON MUST RULE BEFORE THIS RUNG IS BUILT.** The fork: (a) the chain becomes ALLOCATION-ONLY bookkeeping (never a root) and roots come exclusively from live continuations/handles/stack — then dead frames are genuinely unreachable and collectable; or (b) the chain stays a root and frames must be EXPLICITLY unlinked at generator exhaust/cut/determinate-exit (i.e. fix the leak at the source — see the s40 rung ZLS-LEAK), making "on the chain" ≡ "live". These are not equivalent: (a) is a collector, (b) is a discipline. Only after that ruling: dictionary bucket walk; GVA slot walk; suspended-frame/coexpr-handle walk; stack saved-RIP range-check hook (feeds CS-2). **⛔ s31 (Lon directives 2026-07-12, live session): RUNG NARY — A CONSTRUCT EARNS A NODE IFF IT OWNS RUNTIME STATE.** THE DESIGN RULE OF RECORD, ratified by Lon this session and the answer to SU-E's standing concern: **a construct earns an IR node iff it owns runtime state; a stateless construct is an EDGE, not a node.** The distributed SAVE+join shape was a fossil of JCON's linear-bytecode world, where "which alternative is live" had nowhere to live but the program counter; SCRIP has a real graph + ζ frame storage, so encoding runtime state as graph TOPOLOGY is data pretending to be structure. One rule answers the ALT triple, the ARBNO triple, and the GOTO ubiquity at once. Lon's seed `SCRIP/seed/test_sno_1.c` is the SPEC: ONE alt box, `alt_i` index in ζ, β dispatching to the live alternative's OWN β.
**✅ SN4-NARY-ALT LANDED s31 (SCRIP `03e48c13`) — n-ary `IR_MATCH_ALTERNATE`, the SAVE+join triple DELETED.** ONE node A, 2N operands = `(entry_i, resume_i)` pairs; ζ = δ save +0 / dcap save +4 / **alt_i +8** (the PTR_CODE resume continuation is GONE). α: save δ+dcap, alt_i=0, enter entry_0. **β: dispatch on alt_i to the live alternative's OWN resume β** (seed `alt_β`) — this FIXES the documented residual where a nested generator inside an alternative had its remaining ways skipped. fail-glue: alt_i++, reload δ+dcap, dispatch to entry_{alt_i}, else ω. **INSIDE-EDGE TAGS (the disambiguation mechanism, reusable for SEQUENCE):** alternatives are lowered with succ=A, fail=A, then a re-tag pass over each alternative's allocation range marks γ→A as **"σ"** (land the success-glue `na_s`) and ω→A as **"φ"** (land the fail-glue `na_f`; a FAIL-goto's γ→A is also φ). OUTSIDE edges keep their α/β tags and land A's REAL α/β. Consequence: **A is first-allocated, so A.β IS resume and A.ω IS leftward exhaust** — the ALT-EXHAUST CHASE special case in `sno_resume_ω_to` self-disables (`n_operands>0`), and the whole "constructs whose first-allocated ω is not their leftward exhaust" bug class stops growing. `IR_LIT(A).ival = N`, promoted to `_.op_ival` at walk_bb_node's single dispatch point (do NOT set op_ival in the drive case — it is overwritten there; that cost a debug cycle). `x86_asm.h` gained `XK_PAIR` + `PAIR(i)` + `x86_{deflabel,jmp,jcc}_pair` (reusing the existing `'E'`/`'F'` walker arms) so driver-minted glue labels are addressable INDIVIDUALLY, not only via the whole-array `x86_pair_loop()`.

NO dcap machinery and NO δ reload — elements undo their own state through their own β/ω transit (vs ALT, whose alternative-switch abandons wholesale). Same σ/φ inside-edge tags, same 2N+2 pair layout, `flat_drive_match_alt` reused VERBATIM, zero new encoders. **Fences SPLIT the spine** per the design: each maximal fence-free run ≥2 → its own S with the run's shared fail; fence seams stay edge-threaded, `right_sealed` non-repoint preserved verbatim. S first-allocated ⇒ S.β IS the resume surface, S.ω IS leftward exhaust. **MEASURED — composition is honest: `sno_seq_flatten_pat` reduced to SPINE-ONLY** (a parenthesized group = a normal element with its own nested S and a real β); ablation ran BOTH ways, full-flatten and spine-only each watermark-identical at the full crosscheck; the s8 bug shape (`'AB CD' ? **⛔ s32 DESIGN RULING OF RECORD (Lon, live session): TWO-FLAVOR SEQ STORAGE.** The construct's runtime state ("which element is the live choice point") has two representations selected by the ZLS layer, never by template logic: **flat-frame/movable arenas = ARRAY flavor (seq_i index — indices survive a ZH/BBGC slide; live precedent: `rt_zcol_push` is realloc-backed and indexed, never linked)**; **BB-owned rsp = LINKED FRAME CHAIN flavor (header prev-link WRITTEN at element push, TRAVERSED at β/fail-glue; chain depth ≡ the index; raw links safe because rsp frames never move, and REQUIRED because element frames interleave with dynamic-extent residents so no static offsets exist)**. seq_i and the head link are the same datum in two representations. Linked flavor lands with ZB-ITER/ZB-OWN under the `ZLS_FRAME_REGION`/`ZLS_FORTH_CELL` defines (s29). The IR node and glue are flavor-blind by construction.

**✅ SN4-BAREKW + SN4-BAL LANDED s34 (SCRIP `73b6b154` + `b7317175` + `3c30b073` · corpus `f1e13ceb`) — THE SEVEN ARGUMENT-LESS PATTERN KEYWORDS WERE UNREACHABLE FROM THE PARSER.** `pat_prim_kind()` (snobol4.y:37) is consulted at **exactly ONE grammar site — snobol4.y:195, `T_FUNCTION T_LPAREN`** — so it only fires when a name is FOLLOWED BY A PAREN. `ABORT ARB BAL FAIL FENCE REM SUCCEED` take no arguments ⇒ they never reach it; they fall to snobol4.y:196 (`T_IDENT → TT_VAR`) and arrive at the lowerer as **plain variables**. ARB/REM/FENCE had been bandaged by **three strcmp patches inside `sno_pat_node`'s TT_VAR arm**; the other four were never bandaged and lowered to **`IR_MATCH_DEFER(<name>)` = a deferred read of an UNSET VARIABLE**. Oracle-measured consequences: **ABORT = SILENT WRONG ANSWER** (matched where the manual requires failure), **SUCCEED = SILENT WRONG ANSWER**, FAIL = accidentally-right-answer/wrong-mechanism, BAL = wrong. ⇒ **`case TT_ABORT:`/`TT_BAL:`/`TT_SUCCEED:`/`TT_FAIL:` in lower_snobol4.c HAVE NEVER ONCE EXECUTED**, and `IR_MATCH_BAL/FENCE/ABORT` + `bb_match_abort/bb_match_fence` rotted for exactly that reason: **nothing could reach them.** The "incomplete IR_MATCH_* set" was never incomplete — it was **unreachable**. Fix: ONE normalizer `sno_pat_eff_kind()`; `sno_pat_node`/`sno_pat_supported`/`sno_is_pattern_rhs`/`sno_is_fence` all switch on it; the three strcmp bandages DELETED. **Grammar NOT touched** (REPO-SCRIP.md forbids running bison against the committed `.y`/`.tab.c`); manual Ch.18 p.203 licenses the promotion — these are protected built-ins ("these variables cannot be altered"), so they cannot shadow a user variable.
**s34 — THE s31 RULE SPLITS THE SEVEN CLEANLY, AND THE SPLIT IS THE WHOLE DESIGN:** **ABORT/FAIL/FENCE own NO runtime state ⇒ EDGES (wiring)** — and ABORT's pre-existing `GOTO pat_seal` is *already* correct, because **`pat_seal` bypasses `IR_MATCH_HEAD` while ordinary element-fail routes THROUGH it**, and HEAD *is* the anchor-advance loop: that one edge distinction IS the manual's ABORT-vs-FAIL split (p.125, "ABORT stops all pattern matching, while FAIL tells the system to back up and try other alternatives **or other subject starting positions**"). **BAL owns EXTENT ⇒ IT EARNS A NODE** — a generator (p.124/p.203: "matches the shortest string possible", extends on retry). **`IR_MATCH_BAL` LANDED**: structurally ARB + a paren-depth counter, ζ 16B in the **SPAN shape** (+0 n / +4 entry-δ / +8 depth / +12 pad; a pure in-frame generator, **no ζ push ⇒ NO ZLS2 grant**, unlike ARB). Two manual-forced differences pinned in the template: **(1) NON-NULL** — the scan consumes a char BEFORE it may yield, so extent 0 is never a result (ARB *does* yield 0); **(2) it exhausts the moment depth goes NEGATIVE** — a `)` that closes nothing can never be rebalanced by anything to its right, so `)A+B` is not a non-match *at this extent*, it is the **END of the generator**. **α initialises and falls through into the scan; β re-enters the SAME scan with n/depth intact — "resume and extend to the next balanced extent" IS the loop**, so one loop serves both ports and **zero new encoders** were needed.

**⛔ s34 RESIDUE — TWO CONSTRUCTS STILL OPEN, BOTH NOW REFUSED *LOUDLY* RATHER THAN SILENTLY MIS-LOWERED:**
- [ ] **SUCCEED — needs a β→γ, and it is SU-E in miniature.** Manual p.126: "if the scanner is backtracking when it encounters SUCCEED, it reverses and starts forward again." It owns **no stored state**, so the s31 rule says EDGE — but a bare `IR_GOTO` has **no resume surface**. This is the exact question SU-E raises. **Lon's ruling wanted:** does a stateless-but-resumable construct get a node (zero ζ, live β), or does the edge vocabulary grow a β? **Manual-forced sequencing: SUCCEED must land AFTER ABORT** — Coppen's rule 2 (p.127) says SUCCEED without ABORT either never terminates or is superfluous, and the canonical `SUCCEED … FAIL` oscillator only halts via ABORT. ABORT is now landed, so SUCCEED is unblocked. (Forward-direction SUCCEED already measures oracle-green via the fallback; it is the β that is missing.)
- [ ] **FENCE keyword ≠ FENCE(P) function — WE CONFLATE THEM, AND IT IS AN OracLE-MEASURED SILENT DIVERGENCE.** Manual is explicit (p.222 / p.125): keyword FENCE ⇒ backup **fails the match**; **`FENCE(P)` ⇒ backup PASSES THROUGH and does NOT abort** — only the alternatives *inside* P are hidden on backup. `sno_is_fence()` treats both identically and seals both. **The manual's own example diverges:** `P = FENCE(BREAK(',') | REM) $ STR *DIFFER(STR)` on `'abc'` → oracle `STR=abc`, SCRIP **failed**. The seal must become **direction-sensitive**; FENCE(P) stays an edge, just a *different* edge. (Untested-in-corpus; probe kept at /tmp/probe/m_fencefn2.sno — re-derive it, don't trust this line.)
**⚠ s34 — ORPHAN OPCODES, Lon's call (wire or delete):** `IR_MATCH_RETRY`/`IR_MATCH_ADVANCE` are **fossils of a superseded anchor-loop design** — templates exist, nothing lowers to them, and they reference **`kw_anchor` while `IR_MATCH_HEAD` (which absorbed the anchor loop) uses `g_anchor`**: two different symbols, which is the tell. `IR_MATCH_SPAN_VAR` has a template and no lowering; `IR_MATCH_CALLOUT` has nothing at all. Also **`TT_SUCCEED`/`TT_FAIL` are OVERLOADED ACROSS FRONTENDS** — the *Pascal* parser mints `TT_SUCCEED` as its empty-statement/sequence-terminator node (pascal.y:605,636,698). Same kind, two unrelated meanings; harmless today only because the trees never cross.

Cross-phase rule: after EVERY landing re-run the full crosscheck and let the RESULT redraw the remaining steps — delete steps that fixed themselves, split steps that turn out to be two, and say which happened.

## PHASE 1 — ⭐⭐ RUNG NCB — NO-C→BB / ONE-ENTRY CONVENTION (Lon directive 2026-07-11 s22: "Should always be C -> BB -> BB -> BB (C call) -> BB ... RETURN to C in MODE 3! For mode 4 it's just main start.") — PREREQ FOR ZB-OWN

THE CONVENTION (the invariant): MODE 3 has exactly ONE C→BB transfer — the driver MAIN branch (scrip.c ~:1327, `m3_enter_with_rbx(fn, rt_frame(), 0, arena)` / `(void)fn(rt_frame(), 0)`). The graph then runs BB→BB entirely in assembly land; C runtime helpers are STRICT LEAVES (a BB calls C, C returns to that BB, C NEVER calls or jmps into a BB); the graph RETURNS to C exactly once at program end (the xa_flat exit anchor already does this). MODE 4: crt `_start` → libc → `main` = the emitted graph (driver emits prefix "main", scrip.c:1041); same leaf rule, runtime linked in. TARGET END-STATE: NO static r12 frame — r12 (and rbp) free up, ALL control-flow ζ rides the C stack as rsp bumps (x86_asm.h:261's own prediction); heap residue only for genuinely-escaping frames (co-expressions ride their own per-thread stacks — consistent). Co-expression pthread trampoline (rt_coexpr.c:99: loads the 6-reg contract, `jmp *%rax`, fresh thread stack, never returns) is SANCTIONED as a per-thread MAIN — jmp not call, zero C frames beneath. [Recorded s22; Lon may overrule.]

WHY LOAD-BEARING (s22 audit): the C-trampoline convention is the single root under three recorded walls — (1) ZB-OWN-0's "rsp unreachable" finding (C frames interleave above BB frames; zeta_alloc.c:148 predicts exactly this retirement); (2) the SZ-1 blob-β wall (call/ret returns once and is dead; four-port jmp makes β an edge — same primitive as FINDING-2026-07-10-CLAUDE-SN4-BLOB-BETA-RESUME.md); (3) the s12/s18 error-path SEGV interleave class (SZ-7's other face). The s6 "long-lived heap frames" class exists mostly BECAUSE the trampoline forces a C return; expect it to shrink toward coexpr-only for SNOBOL4 once NCB-2 lands.

  V1 `rt_call_named_proc` rt.c:706/:727 · V2 `rt_call_proc_direct` :737/:759 · V3 `rt_call_named_proc_sl` :838/:856 (deterministic user-proc calls, BB→C→`p->fn(fb,0)`→BB)
  V4 `rt_proc_call_gen`(`_h`) :527/:542/:560/:575 (generator α from C) · V5 `rt_proc_resume_frame`(`_h`) :585/:589/:595/:605 (generator β from C)
  V6 `rt_eval_run` runtime_eval.c:63-82 — C-called asm shim `call *%rax` into every fresh EVAL/CODE chain; ζ is a 512-qword C-LOCAL (:120-127) + the :198 alloca
  V7 by_name_dispatch.c:2782 generator pump (rides V4)

**NCB-0 LANDED s23:** `scripts/test_gate_no_c_to_bb.sh` — shape-based (line-drift-proof), groups by enclosing function into V1-V7, reports 7 groups / 13 sites, self-tested to catch NEW violations (UNLEDGERED group + warning), `--strict` exits 1 (ready for NCB-4). ⚠ Uses `grep -a` throughout — core.c reads as binary (UTF-8 Σ/δ identifiers). **LEDGER CORRECTIONS (gate-discovered, supersede the s22 numbers above):** (a) V4's :527 lives in `rt_call_proc_descr` — a FOURTH deterministic-call trampoline, sibling of V1-V3, not part of rt_proc_call_gen (NCB-1 should take it with V1-V3); (b) V7 has FOUR rider sites, not one: by_name_dispatch.c:764 (`rt_call_value_gen_h` α), :772 (`rt_call_value_resume_h` β), :2782+:2785 (`plc_next` α+β); (c) the sanctioned "driver MAIN branch" is TWO mutually-exclusive branches of scrip.c `main` (~:1327 general mode-3, ~:1407 SNOBOL4 pat_flat whole-program), both `goto run_done` — one transfer per run either way.
**⛔⛔ s24 FINDINGS — THREE OF THEM CONTRADICT THE s23 LANDING PLAN BELOW. READ BEFORE CODING; the plan text is kept for its still-valid parts, but where they conflict, s24 wins (each was verified at file:line, not reasoned).**
  **(F1) SNOBOL4 DEFINE procs are `dyn_scope = 1`** (`lower_snobol4.c:1612`; expr pseudo-procs :1636 likewise; only `main` :1558 and pattern procs :1655 are 0). So `rt_call_proc_descr` **bounced straight to `rt_call_named_proc`** (old rt.c:519) for EVERY SNOBOL4 user call — the s23 plan's "start with the descr deterministic arms" would have moved a forwarding stub, not a trampoline. V1 is SNOBOL4's real target. And dyn_scope is CORRECT, not a wart: SPITBOL DEFINE saves formals AND locals on a pushdown stack, restored on RETURN/FRETURN/NRETURN (manual Ch.8 "Local variables") — that IS dynamic scoping, and it is exactly why call/ret may carry it (call nesting ≡ save/restore nesting). **[RESOLVED s24: `rt_proc_call_open` now selects the protocol, so the delegation is gone and the two protocols differ only INSIDE the leaves.]**
  **(F2) `rbp` IS NOT FREE TODAY** — `x86_asm.h:280` `x86_align_save()` returns **rbp** whenever the frame reg is r12 (today's config). The NCB-5 note "rbp … zero current uses" describes the post-`ZC_FRAME_RSP` END STATE, not the present. A call site that anchored old-rsp in rbp by hand would clobber every enclosing graph's rsp anchor. **USE `x86_align_enter()` / `x86_align_leave()`** (x86_asm.h:1062-1069) — the sanctioned dance, already medium-invisible, saves rsp in whichever of {r12,rbp} is not the frame. Bonus: `align_leave` restores rsp from the save reg, so **the frame release IS the anchor restore** — the site never needs `fbytes` again at release, which kills the register-juggling problem outright.
  **(F3) THE PLAN'S TWO-MEDIUM `callproc` ENCODER IS UNNECESSARY.** Because `rt_frame_prep` hands the callee entry back as a runtime VALUE, both media emit the SAME indirect `call rax`. No `call proc_<n>_α` symbol (TEXT) vs table-index (BINARY) split. The only new encoder is **`x86("call", reg)` — indirect call through a register** (TEXT `call rax`; BINARY `FF /2`, +REX for r8-r15): one shape, medium-symmetric, R2-clean by construction. Plan step (iv) is superseded.
  **(F4) THE CALLEE ABI ALREADY ADMITS A BB→BB CALL — the C trampoline contributes NOTHING at the ABI level.** `xa_flat` prologue (xa_flat.cpp:112-130): callee takes **rdi = frame base, esi = entry flag**, SELF-LOADS rbx from `g_gva_base` (:93-109 — the durable CSTACK contract adopted after rt_call_named_proc's alloca let GCC clobber rbx, the 083 segfault), anchors its own rsp, and already emits `cmp esi,0 / jne` — **the β-resume dispatch NCB-2 wants exists in the prologue TODAY**. Callee is `ret`-terminated and restores its entry rsp itself. So `rdi=fb, esi=0, call fn` is a fully legal transfer, and the caller need not preserve rbx for the callee.

**✅ NCB-1a LANDED s24 (SCRIP `cf809632`) — THE LEAF SPLIT.** `rt_proc_call_open(name,nargs) → fbytes|0` · `rt_frame_prep(fb,fbytes) → fn` · `rt_proc_call_epilogue(fret) → result`. All three deterministic trampolines (V1 `rt_call_named_proc`, V2 `rt_call_proc_direct`, V4's `rt_call_proc_descr`) now read as EXACTLY the emitted site's sequence — open → frame → transfer → epilogue — with the frame still C-made (alloca/zls2) and the transfer still a C indirect call. Cross-transfer state (save_base, Σ, want-name, rname, p, fb, protocol) rides a **LIFO pcall context stack, NOT C locals** — so the emitted site needs **no LOWER ζ grant** to hold it, side-stepping the `drive_value_slot` landmine entirely. Zero codegen change ⇒ watermark byte-identical (m3 284/7, m4 283/7/1, DIVERGE=1/1017; sno 7/7×2, icon 12/12×2, prolog 5/5×2). Also drops a linear proc scan from the descr hot path (rt_proc_find is cached). **V3 `rt_call_named_proc_sl` was NOT folded in — it is a genuinely different shape** (static link in `fb[0]`, args bound into frame slots, only the result name saved); it needs its own `open` flavor, or leave it C until it has a live caller worth moving.
**✅ NCB-1b LANDED s25 (SCRIP `4e84e986`) — THE CALL SITE IS EMITTED.** `bb_call_proc_staged.cpp`'s two deterministic arms collapse into ONE R2 concat (`bcps_det_arm`) that IS the site: `align_enter → stage args → rt_proc_call_open → sub rsp,rax (THE FRAME) → rt_frame_prep → call rax ⭐ → rt_proc_call_epilogue → align_leave`. Deterministic SNOBOL4 proc calls now run **C → BB → BB** with no C trampoline. Watermark IDENTICAL both modes (m3 284/7, m4 283/7/1, DIVERGE=1/1017, same failing set); sno 7/7×2, icon 12/12×2, prolog 5/5×2. `.s` artifacts regenerated (11 feature + 5 benchmark; demos unchanged — they still FATAL at tree-kind 25, see the stale-artifact finding below). **THE ARM (LANDED s25 — `bcps_det_arm`; REUSE IT VERBATIM for NCB-1c/1d, no new encoder needed):**
  ```
  x86_align_enter()                      ; save rsp in {r12,rbp}\frame; 16-align. THE anchor for the whole window.
  [arg staging FOR-loop: unchanged — rt_arg_stage into g_call_args]
  mov rdi, <name>  ; mov esi, <nargs>    ; RO load: x86_ro_load_q(rdi,0) + x86_ro_seal_str(0,name) trailer
  call rt_proc_call_open                 ; rax = fbytes, or 0 = no body
  test rax, rax ; je L(1)                ; internal label (landed 2026-06-02)
  sub rsp, rax                           ; THE FRAME. rax is 16-aligned, rsp was 16-aligned ⇒ stays aligned.
  mov rdi, rsp ; mov rsi, rax            ; fbytes OUT of rax BEFORE the call — x86_call_ro clobbers rax
  call rt_frame_prep                     ; fills+binds per protocol; rax = callee entry
  mov rdi, rsp ; xor esi, esi            ; fb, entry=0 (α)
  call rax                               ; ⭐ THE BB→BB TRANSFER — x86("call", reg), ALREADY EXISTED (F5)
  mov rdi, rax ; mov rsi, rdx            ; fret (DESCR in rax:rdx → passed in rdi:rsi; gcc marshals it the same)
  call rt_proc_call_epilogue             ; rax:rdx = result; pops the ctx
  jmp L(2)
  L(1):  call rt_faildescr               ; rax:rdx = FAILDESCR (leaf added s25)
  L(2):  x86_align_leave()               ; rsp = anchor ⇒ FRAME RELEASED FOR FREE; save-reg restored
  [store rax/rdx → FRQ(off)/FRQ(off+8); cmp eax,99; je ω; γ; β — all unchanged]
  ```
  ⚠ **EVERY exit from the window must pass through `align_leave`** (hence the two internal labels): skipping it leaves the save register holding OUR rsp instead of the enclosing graph's entry rsp, and the graph's epilogue then restores the WRONG rsp. This is the one way to get this shape badly wrong.
  ⚠ The generator arms (`bcps_*_gen_arm`) are STILL the forbidden shape — they are NCB-2's, deliberately left.

Blob ABI `(fb, entry)` UNCHANGED this rung — only the caller side moves; the rt_call_* bodies shrink to leaf registrars. Assembly `call/ret` is a legal BB→BB transfer for DETERMINISTIC procs (RETURN/FRETURN once); generators wait for NCB-2. Gate: full crosscheck both modes watermark-identical; ledger 7→4. DCR precedent applies.
