  (i) **TWO live caller templates, both need the rework:** `bb_call.cpp` (dispatched emit.cpp:816; picks `rt_call_named_proc`/`_sl`/`rt_call_arr` at :204-:209) and `bb_call_proc_staged.cpp` (op `IR_CALL_PROC_STAGED`, IR.h:32; deterministic arms call `rt_call_proc_descr`, gen arms call `rt_proc_call_gen_h`/`rt_proc_resume_frame_h` — the gen arms are NCB-2's, leave them). ⚠ BOTH templates are pre-revamp legacy with hand-written `*_bin_arm`/`*_txt_arm` pairs (the FORBIDDEN SHAPE, standing pointer above) — the NEW call-proc encoder must be ONE medium-invisible `x86(...)` form; converting the whole templates to R2 is BB-FIXUP scope, do NOT let it balloon this rung.
  (ii) **Trampoline decomposition (rt_call_named_proc rt.c:706, the model — all four do the same 16 steps):** find → resolve_cells → fbytes=max(nest,frame_bytes) 16-aligned → `rt_name_save_push` formals (manual Ch.8: formals AND locals saved LIFO, restored on return — call/ret nesting preserves this exactly, the semantic proof the move is safe) → result-name push unless formal-shadowed → alloca/zls2 frame → memset 0 → save Σ/Σlen C-globals → monitor call event → klevel++ → `p->fn(fb,0)` → klevel-- → restore Σ → release → result (FAILDESCR | rcell | NV_GET(rname)) → `rt_nret_fix` (NRETURN, manual Ch.9) → `rt_name_restore` → monitor return event. SPLIT: prologue leaf (find..monitor-call, returns proc entry ptr with `{fn, frame_bytes}` readable) + epilogue leaf (restore-Σ..monitor-return, takes fb + saved bases). `rt_frame_bind_args` EXISTS (rt.c:490, static — export it or fold into the prologue leaf). Arg staging (`rt_arg_stage`) stays as-is, precedes the prologue.
  (iii) **Frame:** fbytes is RUNTIME data (DEFINE executes at runtime) — emitted site loads it from the prologue-returned entry, `sub rsp` dynamic, saves old rsp in the caller's own zls slot for the release; memset moves into `rt_frame_bind_args`.
  (iv) **Encoder:** one new `x86("callproc", ...)` shape in x86_asm.h — TEXT emits `call proc_<name>_α` (symbol convention proven at scrip.c:729/:1130), BINARY emits indexed indirect through the proc table; medium switched INSIDE the encoder (R2/R7/R9/R10).

## ⭐ RUNG ZS — ALL ζ ON THE STACK (Lon directive 2026-07-14 s57; precondition for the unified GC heap)
**LAW STATUS (corrected s57-afternoon, Lon: "IR_DEFER does not need to allocate much of anything except maybe the DESCR that points to DT_E. The BBs do their own allocation — just jump to the BBs and they allocate for themselves. No pre-allocating necessary."):** the s57-morning runtime-sized-cell/mark-restore amendment is RETRACTED — the 2026-07-12 fixed-cell law stands UNAMENDED. DEFER's cell is FIXED 16 bytes (the DESCR holding DT_E / the resolved target), an ordinary fc_geom grant like SPAN/ARB. The monolithic pre-allocation was an artifact of the call/ret transfer, not a storage need.
**DT_E FACT (manual p.189, verbatim semantics):** an EXPRESSION is evaluated "when E is referenced in some subsequent pattern match (or EVAL'ed)" — the match-time dereference of DT_E and EVAL(E) are ONE operation, explicit vs implicit, all LIFO. ZS-3 makes them one emitted site.

**THE SEVEN REDS ARE NOT SEVEN BUGS.** Six (SZ-1..SZ-6) are ONE architectural wall wearing six faces: **a pattern living inside a compiled blob cannot have its internal generator state resumed, nor its speculative captures undone, from OUTSIDE that blob** — which is exactly the C-call-convention wall NCB-2 retires. SZ-7 is genuinely unrelated (error-path mode disagreement). The step boundaries are a HYPOTHESIS about how the class decomposes: if a step clears more than its own tests, DELETE the steps it cleared rather than pretending they were separately earned.

Each step: read the SPITBOL manual for the constructs it touches → 2-way IPC sync-step monitor to bracket first-divergence ↔ last-agreeing → gdb spin-counter breakpoint in the bracket → single-step to the land mine → one targeted fix → both modes vs `.ref` → full crosscheck for regressions. ## PHASE 3 — ⭐ RUNG ZB-OWN — BB-OWNED ζ MM, STACK-FIRST (Lon directive 2026-07-11: "switch over to using ZETA local storage on the stack and BB-owned ... if BB's own their own allocation, then worries about when to create activation blocks is gone") — **NCB (Phase 1) IS THE PREREQ**

DESIGN (full record: `ARCH-ZETA-LOCAL-STORAGE.md` §7): each BB generated with g_emit switches between TWO MM flavors — ζ-on-STACK (α decrements, ω increments; grows down) and ζ-on-HEAP (α bumps, ω marks-as-garbage; the ZH collector slides). Today's black-and-white BB-owned vs caller-owned split becomes a HYBRID: any straight branch-delimited sequence of BBs COALESCES into a region — a separate ENTER BB allocates for itself and the whole block with recalculated offsets; interior boxes' alloc/free suppressed; region exits free. BB-owned = region-of-one; today's caller-alloc flat frame = the maximal region — re-derived as an OPTIMIZER THEOREM, not an emitter assumption (the WALL rule applied to storage). LIFETIME RULE (s6, carried): control-flow-lifetime frames → stack flavor; frames that ESCAPE control flow (IR_SUSPEND, IR_CREATE, IR_PROC_GEN, blob-carrying DEFER) → heap flavor (ZH seam) — per-KIND static knowledge LOWER has, promoted through g_emit; expect the escape set to SHRINK post-NCB-2 (no forced C return). KNOWN COSTS + answers: (1) γ never frees → success-direction accumulation (the fib/tak/queens ZH blocker) → bounded by the statement backstop, KILLED at ZB-OWN-4. (2) cross-BB operand slots (op_sa) assume one shared frame → regions are exactly the unit inside which slot-passing stays frame-relative; boundary-crossers land in the parent region's frame.

**ZB-OWN-0 LANDED s21 (`be4bb739`, `ZC_PORT_OWNED=5`, opt-in, default byte-identical) — the balance-proving shadow. ⛔ Lon s22: NO SHADOW WANTED — treat as scaffolding; ZB-OWN-1 makes it real; delete/absorb the shadow arm as boxes start reading the cursor.** Its two findings STAND: (i) rsp unreachable while C trampoline frames interleave — **retired by NCB, not by cleverness here**; (ii) **NEVER-HOIST COMPOSITION RULE**, proven live: an unconditional absolute store turned 25 ARB/ARBNO/capture tests red on one shared cursor; floor-only (store iff strictly below; dynamic RELEASE restores the ceiling) took =5 back to watermark. Residue: name the 9 =5 m4 skips before rung 1; optional INSTRUMENTED-style assert flavor (cell vs expected C_i at α); `rt_zeta_cstack()` stayed CSTACK-only — revisit when OWNED grows a real backing store.

- [ ] **ZB-OWN-1 — addressing switchover for box-LOCAL scratch.** Tag zls fields box-local vs cross-read (zeta_storage.c already names every field); box-local quads address off the OWNED cursor (rsp-relative post-NCB) instead of `FR(r12-off)`; cross-read (value/operand-edge) quads stay in the shared frame. This tagging is ALSO the data-edge half of the region partition (the scout classifies control edges only). Gate: crosscheck under =5; grep proves no template reads another node's local.
- [ ] **ZB-OWN-2 — lifetime split, heap flavor.** Escape kinds route their frame to ZH (`rt_zh` alpha-bump / omega-mark) via the existing `--zeta=zh` seam; stack flavor for the rest. Re-derive the escape set POST-NCB (it should be near-empty for SNOBOL4). Gate: suspend/coexpr/gen-proc suite green under OWNED+zh.
- [ ] **ZB-OWN-3 — region coalescing pass (`src/optimizer/`). SCOUT LANDED s21 (`a039442f`): `src/optimizer/region_report.c`, `SCRIP_REGION_REPORT=1`** — read-only partition + edge classifier; per-region span / external γ+ω in/out / β-sigil-in; verdict GAMMA-FREE-SAFE (β-in==0 ⇒ frame dead past its γ) vs HELD. Evidence: 104 fence-seal reports β-in=0 (FENCE semantics ARE γ-free safety, mechanically); 052 ARBNO HEAD reports HELD. Computable only because of s21's uniform-β wiring (every backtrack edge is a sigil-marked IR edge — edge scan, not template archaeology). ⛔ **TWO RESTOREs — DO NOT CONFLATE (Lon s21):** (a) cursor-ARITHMETIC restore at β: RETIRED (s7 ruling; ω landing on a β already put the shared cursor right). (b) **REGISTER-restore at β: REQUIRED** in the region model — boxes address `[ζ_reg + local_off]`, the region entrance repoints ζ_reg, so a β arriving from ANOTHER region re-establishes: `mov ζ, PARENT_FRQ(region_slot)` (the ARB/ARBNO save-slot pattern). rbp is the candidate ζ_reg (NCB-5). DESIGN (Lon s21): subgraphs are VIEWS over the ONE stored graph; single-entry/single-exit (α/γ) via a synthetic ENTER/handshake box; entrance sets ζ_reg until it jumps out; inter-region branches are external jumps. Natural seeds: procs/functions, whole statements, pattern portions. This is CHUNKS, recovered. ⚠ **v0 FINDING:** seeds must NOT be raw allocation intervals (`MATCH_HEAD..MATCH_RELEASE` is 2 nodes — the bracket allocates before its body); v1 seeds = the operand-recorded ranges the lowerer leaves behind (ARBNO body span operand pairs; ALT/capture T/F adjacency) + γ-threading — never `g->all[]` order. REMAINING: ENTER allocates the coalesced block; interior α/β positioning suppressed; offsets region-relative; exits free; regions nest, boundary-crossers promoted to parent. Gate: crosscheck identical at EVERY coalescing level (dial 0=BB-owned … max = today's flat frame byte-for-byte — the theorem). LINEAGE: this is the old "step 3 chain-coalesce" of the retired ⭐⭐⭐ BB-OWNED-ζ section (steps 1/2 DONE/PROVEN cont.-3..5b, see LANDED DIGEST).
- [ ] **ZB-OWN-4 — γ-edge liveness release.** Regions provably dead past their γ free on success too; kills det-success accumulation; re-evaluate ZH as compiled default (removes the fib/tak/queens blocker note in zeta_choices.h).

WATCH-ITEM: C-stack depth — deep ARBNO/recursion sized for a 512MB arena may not fit an 8MB C stack; guard-page fault is the free bomb; add a `ulimit -s` note to the gate before making OWNED default.

⛔ STANDING ω FACTS (from the retired ⭐⭐⭐ section — do not re-derive): (1) **ω is NOT uniformly "the death"** — bb_match_arbno emits SIX `jmp ω` across six roles; only roles 2 (v1) and 5 (v2) are true irreversible exits; a universal port==OMEGA free = silent downstream corruption. Frees are gated by **zls2_geom GRANTS** (role-static death knowledge from the one layout authority), never by a chain classifier. (2) `op_omega_is_death` (emit.cpp ~1514) is BROKEN for role-2-when-MATCH_HEAD-shares-the-chain-window (verified `SCRIP_OMEGA_DIAG`) — flagged, unfixed; trust it for nothing. (3) Single-exit markers exist: role 2 `def L(9)`, role 5 `L(2)` — one greppable address per true death. (4) **r12 cannot be repointed per-construct** (breaks every sibling `[r12+off]`) — stands today; dissolves at ZB-OWN-3 where the region ENTER repoints ζ_reg once per region and β re-establishes.

## PHASE 5 — PERF (post-green; A/B every claim, byte-identical gates throughout)

- [ ] **NRG — Native Register Codegen ("beat the interpreter on statements").** THESIS: SPITBOL is indirect-threaded; SCRIP loses on statements because it replaced threading's one-indirect-branch NEXT with a full PLT call per primitive; the only win is specialization threading can't do — proven-typed values resident in registers, raw ops, no per-op tagged-descriptor reload. (Patterns are the separate battleground.) Order **A→E→B→C→D**: **NRG-A** VN-0 value-number repeated `[rbx+k*16]` loads + tag-checks in straight-line chains; VN-1 drop store-then-reload + dead IR_VAR · **NRG-E** BOPT-0 port→field table (read-only); BOPT-1 wire branch chaining as LOWER→EMITTER pass + cycle guard (⛔ honor the prior crash: the γ-spine doubles as operand-recovery structure — rechain only edges proven not to shorten that walk; session-27 in-place mutation attempt CLOSED, writeup `docs/BOPT-DESIGN.md`, `src/opt/branchopt.{c,h}` kept as dead-end record); BOPT-2 dead-BB drop + fall-through layout; BOPT-3 copy propagation · **NRG-B** REG-0 liveness + single-type analysis (reuse FZ invariance + GVA DT_I guard); REG-1 counter in a callee-saved reg across a loop, spill at exit/escape edges, re-materializable at every β/ω; REG-2 DT_I function-body locals · **NRG-C** INL-0 inlinability analysis (≤N stmts, non-recursive, `__proc[]` proves the static set); INL-1 splice callee four-port graph at call site · **NRG-D** PROTO-0 per-call-site block save/restore through known cells (needs the **nv set** ledger stamp, still only requested). Gates per sub-rung: crosscheck byte-identical + both-medium + regen touched `.s` + A/B wall-ms.
- [ ] **DEFER-ZLS — kill bb_match_defer's per-match `rt_zls_alloc(65536)`/release pair** (the cost FZ-5 exposed; the real stored-pattern hot-loop rung). Candidates: ride MATCH_HEAD's statement-scope mark/release backstop, or a lazily-grown reusable arena. ⚠ COORDINATE with NCB-2/SZ-1 — they rewrite the same template. Also owed: the pattern-tail attribution A/B (`--zeta-port=plain` vs CSTACK) before blaming any single cause for the 600×-class tails.
- [ ] **SAB — string self-append capacity** (string_concat 5.6×, string_manip 30× behind official SPITBOL). `S = S X` mallocs+copies O(n) per iteration. SAB-0 detect `V = V <expr>` in lower · SAB-1 string-builder cell `(ptr,len,cap)` runtime type + emit arm · SAB-2 ⛔ correctness-critical: preserve copy-on-assign value semantics (any read/alias of V sees an immutable snapshot) · SAB-3 gate + A/B.
- [ ] **EVR — EVAL/CODE arena reclaim** (eval_dynamic 246× behind official; fresh baseline s10-b: ~94µs/eval full pipeline, 100% cache miss by benchmark design). Fix: snapshot BB pool top before `eval_build_chain`, run, restore (mprotect RX→RW), free per-call AST/IR. NOTE: NCB-3 restructures the INVOKE, not the reclaim — both still wanted. See BBGC for the general version.
- [ ] **CFOLD-0/1 — literal-expression fold in lower** (feeds BOPT: folded always-true/false relop → unconditional edge → dead-branch prune). ⛔ Fold only literal + side-effect-free + oracle-exact (integer width/overflow, number↔string coercion, the null-string-identity rule already fixed once in `str_concat_d`) — verify each folded form against the oracle individually. Includes the s18 follow-ons: literal-arg predicate folding (`GT(2,1)`), TAB/POS literal-negative range checks (oracle code 184), user-DEFINE shadow lift for the 12 intercepted relop names (consult defs[] before intercepting).
- [ ] **PEEP-0/1 — peephole + within-BB CSE** on the structured op-list before flatten (redundant spill/restore, `mov reg,reg`, dead stores, already-live lea/mov; CSE complements BOPT-3).
- [ ] **GVA-4 — rbp GST hash-path skip for `$X` runtime indirect refs** (optimization only; GVA-0 forwarding already guarantees correctness). Marginal; after everything above.
- [ ] **FOLD BLOB TIER** — `IR_REF_INVARIANT` string tier landed; blob tier remains.
- [ ] **FURTHER ZLS CONSUMERS** (REM/FENCE/SPAN/BREAK/BREAKX/SPAN_VAR onto the per-activation protocol) — ONLY with a live overlap exerciser, i.e. after DP-7 lands recursive `*P`; converting earlier buys uniformity, not correctness (recorded FINDING).
- [ ] **BBGC — slide-compaction GC for the BB code arena.** ⬇ LOW PRIORITY / EXPLORATORY — do NOT start ahead of the above. BBGC-0 measure (EVAL/CODE stress that exhausts the bound) · 1 root enumerator · 2 entry-table handle indirection · 3 mark · 4 safepoint discipline · 5 sweep+slide+re-stitch the four ports (everything else in a sealed blob needs zero fixup under a move) · 6 trigger on overflow · 7 gate. Hazard: stored blob-entry pointers outside the pool (EVAL cache, DT_C/DT_E, label→code map, live return addresses) — handles + no-active-BB-frame safepoints.
- SCAN ladders, PARKED: **SCAN-FOLD-0/1** literal-arg seal audit + close gaps (only active scan rung besides GEM — GEM-0/1 unblocked per s18 finding ii, operand-edge+hoist supersedes the old fetch-by-name descriptions; `bb_match_span_var`'s `rt_nv_cstr` is do-not-copy and now unreachable, dead-code candidate); SCAN-FOLD-2 NSPAN parked · **SCAN-TABLE-0/1/2** baked 256-byte classifier (char MUST be unsigned; runtime-cset built-once table = the real win: `BREAK(WORD) SPAN(WORD)` in wordcount/claws5/treebank) · **SCAN-UNROLL-0/1** ≤~10-char inline compares.

## ⛔ STANDING FACTS & FINDINGS — read once, never re-derive

**DOCTRINE:** SNOBOL4 rides the same pipeline as Icon — `sno_parse_ast → lower_sno_stage2 → shared optimizer → ir_drive_slot_assign → the ONE language-blind emitter → mode-3/mode-4`. SCRIP follows SPITBOL semantics; oracle = `/home/claude/x64/bin/sbl -b`. Five-phase statement model: (1) build subject; (2) build pattern; (3) perform pattern; (4) build replacement; (5) perform replacement.

**SN4-PAT MODEL (the feature spine, fully landed through DEFER-CALLOUT v1):** `IR_MATCH_*` (matchers, inline needle, γ=success/ω=failure) + `IR_PATTERN_*` (STITCH boxes only — per-element pre-D7 builders ABANDONED, do not resurrect) + `IR_REF_INVARIANT` (sealed blob for VARIANT-free subpatterns). B-RE (s16): DT_P.p → head `{fn, rcp}`; VARIANT parts build RECIPES (tree kinds verbatim), first match compiles ONCE via `sno_pat_tree_graph_rt`→optimizer→slot-assign→`emit_chain`; composition composes RECIPES; opaque-fn composition refuses loudly (= SZ-5's guard). Open B-RE residue: recipe TT_ARBNO/TT_FENCE in EXPRESSION position have recipe arms but NO sx routing (deliberate; own probes when landed). Load-bearing landmines: `_.x86_scratch_off` read-before-granted class — grant in `ir_drive_slot_assign` BEFORE wiring any new scratch consumer; **`drive_value_slot` aborts/garbages without a LOWER grant — "add the grant in LOWER, never patch it in emit"** (s21: an emitter-side call on an ungranted node silently corrupted 90+ tests); `IR_lit` is an anonymous union (sval/ival/dval overlap — never write two members); the match-family `push rbx; and rsp,-16` C-call dance poisons the GVA base if the callee re-enters emitted GVA-reading code (touched templates swept to rbp; others inert only while their callees never re-enter — NCB makes this moot); emit's chain-BFS must follow ω for the whole match-family range (ω-only backtrack targets); `DESCR.slen` is ADVISORY everywhere (writers don't maintain it; test NUL-termination). Uniform-β (s21-a) is LOAD-BEARING: every box is on the backtrack chain — a "worked before" that depended on generator-only β wiring was masking, not correctness. `sno_resume_ω_to` convention: a construct's resume surface = its subgraph's FIRST-ALLOCATED node.

**PARK-NEVER-DELETE (standing directive, Lon) — repo-wide.** Park parsed-out code out of the Makefile; never delete (`8f3e4b23` precedent). `src/lower/lower_snobol4.gz5-parked-41b53078.c` = the recovered pre-GZ#5 pattern lowerer; dead-code sweeps must exempt `*-parked-*` files and the on-disk template inventory.

**ORACLES & BENCHMARKS (s13, standing):** fork oracles are TAINTED-BY-DESIGN (fork sbl: `systm.c` TIME()→CPU-ms patch, needed by the monitor — keep for monitor work; fork csnobol4: FENCE(P)+STNO patches + a real unanchored-ANY engine defect). OFFICIALS built+certified in `/home/claude/official/` via `scripts/build_official_oracles.sh` (READ ITS HEADER; csnobol4 = two-stage bootstrap, FIXED-POINT CERTIFIED). Benchmarks/semantics arbitration use officials; rerun via `util_bench_snobol4_engines.sh` (divisors: official sbl 1000000, fork 1, csn 1) + `test_bench_snobol4_modes.sh TIMEOUT=120`. **s13 table (SCRIP m4 ms | official sbl | official csn | ratio):** arith_loop 605|23|134|26× · eval_dynamic 92665|377|1282|246× · eval_fixed 2169|260|721|8.3× · fibonacci 2129|91|539|23× · func_call 7575|410|2452|18× · func_call_overhead 7570|415|2470|18× · mixed_workload 3890|99|475|39× · op_dispatch 1253|67|380|19× · pattern_bt 22405|508|571|44× · roman 3304|122|1066|27× · string_concat 867|156|321|5.6× · string_manip 11187|376|1718|30× · string_pattern 27255|389|1869|70× · table_access 4400|193|2640|23× · var_access 6079|643|3734|9.4× — **⚠ the old "pattern_bt 0.77×, SCRIP beats SPITBOL" claim DOES NOT REPRODUCE** (either regression-since or different-build-then; `-O0` runtime UNPROVEN factor); older cross-engine numbers in corpus/BENCHMARKS.md match TODAY'S officials — the stale side was SCRIP's.

**MONITOR (landed, reference-only):** MON-RE-0..6 done. `g_monitor_bin` flag (zero overhead unset), LABEL/VALUE/CALL/RETURN taps emitted into the flattener, `scripts/test_monitor_2way_sync_step_bin.sh` / `test_monitor_3way_sync_step_auto.sh`. m3 taps reinstated s16-b (`bc_stamped` protects statement anchors from goto-threading; GVA routes through instrumented NV_SET under monitor). Known residue: spl-vs-scr statement-numbering skew under `-INCLUDE` (harness alignment confounded) — follow-on, not blocking single-file hunts. Offline twin `harness/probe/probe.py` needs &STLIMIT/&DUMP honored (neither mode does yet).

**STANDING RESIDUE (small, real, none currently red):** `&STNO` inside a DEFINE body reports the body-local index · `&STLIMIT` fixed −1; negative-STLIMIT STCOUNT nuance unmodeled · `&ERRTEXT` unpopulated on EVAL/CODE failure · DEFINE inside a fragment = loud fatal · fragment graphs skip the &STNO hook · computed `$(expr)` goto fatal (name-based `$X` works) · user DEFINE named `LBL__X` collides with the pseudo-proc namespace · NRETURN: conditional-assignment target `PAT . ## ⬜ RUNG JF — JUMP-FALLTHROUGH ELISION + (POSSIBILITY ONLY) AN INSTRUCTION-ARRAY FINAL STAGE (minted s158, Lon question — **NOT RATIFIED, NOT SCHEDULED, DO NOT START WITHOUT A LON DIRECTIVE**)

**Lon's question, verbatim in substance:** *how much are we losing just on adjacent BB jumps that should just be
fall-through?* — plus the observation that **port logic is emitter-owned and SEPARATE from template bodies**, so the
jump could be elided at the port funnel without touching a single template; and the larger POSSIBILITY of having
`x86()` build an ARRAY of instructions that a final stage reads after a PEEPHOLE pass, instead of templates
emitting TEXT/BINARY directly.

### JF-0 — THE MEASUREMENT (done s158, static; report it before anyone budgets time here)

Static census of real compiler output (`--compile`, claws5 and treebank-list, -O0):

| file | instrs | uncond `jmp` | ADJACENT (fall-through-able) | → pure trampoline | combined |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| claws5 | 5058 | 813 (16.1%) | 67 (1.3%) | 14 (0.3%) | **1.6%** |
| treebank-list | 7341 | 868 (11.8%) | 82 (1.1%) | 11 (0.1%) | **1.3%** |

**READ THESE THREE CONCLUSIONS TOGETHER — the headline number is not the interesting one:**

1. **Unconditional jumps are 12–16% of ALL emitted instructions — but only ~8–9% of them are adjacent.** The
   other ~91% are genuine transfers to non-adjacent targets. Fall-through elision CANNOT touch those; converting
   them would need BLOCK REORDERING (lay each box's γ-successor next), which is a strictly larger rung and is
   where the remaining 10–15% actually lives. **Elision alone buys ~1.3–1.6% of static instructions. Say that
   plainly; do not sell this as attacking the 16%.**
2. **The trampoline count is ALREADY LOW (14/11) because s154's H1b SEQ-GLUE FOLD landed** and chases aliases at
   every jmp funnel (`DRIVE_FILL` γ/ω, `emit_jmp_label`, `bb_emit_patch_rel32`, `x86_pair_tgt`). The cheap
   chain-collapse money was largely taken a session ago; JF is picking up what that left.
3. ⛔ **STATIC ≠ DYNAMIC, AND THE CURRENT BOTTLENECK IS NOT INSTRUCTIONS.** The box histogram put `xchain` at
   ~33% of treebank's time (60.4→32.8 after the carry arm), so chain-jump machinery is far heavier dynamically
   than its static share. BUT s157's fence A/B measured **I refs +2.0% (MORE instructions) with wall 4.75× FASTER**
   — treebank is bound by compulsory write misses from unreleased frames, NOT by instruction count. **⇒ Expect a
   1.3% instruction cut to produce ~0 measurable wall on treebank.** The honest target for JF is **claws5 and
   json**, which s157 explicitly calls "a DIFFERENT disease (no collapse)" — i.e. plausibly front-end-bound, and
   already the two closest to the ≤0.50 goal.

⚠ **s156 ALIGNMENT FACT RULE APPLIES WITH FULL FORCE.** Deleting 5-byte `jmp`s shifts every subsequent byte, and
*"wall-clock A/B on a small codegen change measures CODE ALIGNMENT, not the change"* (eliding 17 bytes/prologue
produced a reproducible 10% claws5 "regression" that was pure alignment). **A LAYOUT-HELD PAD LANE
(`SCRIP_SLOT0_PAD` is the reference impl: replace each elided jmp with 5 nop bytes) plus cachegrind counters are
MANDATORY BEFORE ANY WALL NUMBER IS QUOTED.** Without the pad lane this rung cannot be measured at all, only
believed.

### JF-1 — CHEAP ARM: FALL-THROUGH ELISION AT THE PORT FUNNEL (no template edits, no architecture change)

This is Lon's own observation and it needs none of the array work. Ports are resolved by `bb_emit_x86`'s in-band
record walk (`J <port>` = rel32 patch, `D <port>` = define here), so the emitter already knows both sides.

- [ ] **JF-1a** In the `J`-record handler: if the port's resolved target == the current emission offset (i.e. the
      next thing emitted IS the target label), emit **zero bytes** instead of `E9 rel32`. BINARY arm first.
      Templates are untouched — R2/R7/R9 hold, the `x86("jmp", PORT_GAMMA)` call site does not change. **This is
      the whole point of ports being emitter-owned: the template says "go to γ", the emitter decides γ is next.**
- [ ] **JF-1b** TEXT arm must agree (R10): suppress the same ` jmp LABEL` line so BINARY and TEXT stay 1:1. Verify
      with `as` on before/after `.s`.
- [ ] **JF-1c** Kill-switch `SCRIP_JMP_FALLTHRU=0` ⇒ byte-identical to pristine (md5 the default `.s`), plus the
      **PAD LANE** `SCRIP_JMP_FALLTHRU_PAD=1` (5 nops, layout held) for honest A/B.
- [ ] **JF-1d** Gates: crosscheck ×3 lanes (default/elide/pad) at watermark BOTH modes; smokes 7/7×2; 10 demos
      3-way; `.s` regen ×3 (RULES step 4 — codegen IS touched). Then rail claws5+json same-moment with the pad
      lane, cachegrind Ir/D1 counters quoted alongside.

### JF-2 — THE POSSIBILITY: `x86()` BUILDS AN INSTRUCTION ARRAY; A FINAL STAGE READS IT AFTER PEEPHOLE

**Status: EXPLORATORY. Written because Lon asked for the shape, explicitly "not sure we will do it."**

The good news is this is **evolution, not rewrite**: R9's in-band tagged record stream (`L` bytes / `J` port /
`D` define / `E`,`F` pair-loop) is ALREADY an instruction array in embryo — it is simply consumed once, as a
string, by `bb_emit_x86`. JF-2 makes it first-class and addressable so a pass can rewrite it BEFORE a medium
writer runs.

- [ ] **JF-2a SURVEY / DECISION MEMO (do this and STOP — it is the go/no-go).** Cost the change honestly against
      §JF-0's ~1.3–1.6%: how many `x86_*` encoders, how many record kinds, what the array element must carry
      (mnemonic id, operands, medium-invariant patch intent, provenance box+port for debugging). **Recommend
      no-go unless the array also unlocks BLOCK REORDERING (the other ~91% of jumps) — the array's value is the
      reordering and the peephole it enables, NOT the fall-through elision, which JF-1 already gets for free.**
- [ ] **JF-2b** Element type + `x86()` returns/appends records instead of concatenating a string. The FACT RULES
      survive unchanged and in fact get STRONGER: "ONE MEDIUM, INVISIBLE" becomes structural (there is no medium
      at build time at all — the array is medium-free by construction, and TEXT/BINARY become two readers of one
      array). `bb_bin_t` stays abolished; positions are still DISCOVERED, now by the final stage.
- [ ] **JF-2c** Final stage = two writers over the array (BINARY bytes / GAS text), byte-identical to today with
      the peephole disabled. **This is the safety property that makes the whole rung testable: peephole OFF must
      reproduce the current `.s` byte-for-byte.**
- [ ] **JF-2d** PEEPHOLE pass between array and writers. First rules: drop `jmp` to next; collapse `jmp`→`jmp`
      chains (belt-and-braces vs the s154 fold); drop `mov r,r`; fuse redundant reloads of the same frame slot.
- [ ] **JF-2e** BLOCK REORDERING (the actual prize — attacks the ~91% that JF-1 cannot): order boxes so each
      box's hottest successor is adjacent, turning real transfers into fall-throughs. Needs the array plus a
      successor-frequency source. **This is where the 12–16% becomes reachable; without it JF-2 is not worth it.**
- [ ] **JF-2f** Gates as JF-1d, plus md5 proof that peephole-OFF output == pristine.

**⛔ CROSS-CUTTING:** this touches the SHARED emitter and `x86_asm.h`, so it is NOT SNOBOL4's to land alone —
all five `GOAL-*-BB` sessions collide here exactly as the s-30e8422 keystone did. If ratified, hoist JF-2 into
`GOAL-IR-IMMUTABLE-EMIT.md` / `ARCH-EMITTER.md` and land the shared edit ONCE, dedicated, with every session aware.
